The Mahalanobis cosine similarity (MCS) between two directions, which reweights the inner product by test data covariance, is a natural task-aware refinement. Ying et al. (2026) report that a probe's MCS to a reference probe trained on the out-of-distribution (OOD) data near-perfectly linearly predicts the probe's OOD AUROC (R^2 = 0.98). Here, we extend this empirical finding across models, layers, and concept domains, and prove this general phenomenon in closed form: For balanced classes whose projections are Gaussian, OOD AUROC and MCS to the reference probe are linear because both are sigmoid-shaped functions of the probe's signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) on the test data. The theory also predicts when this linearity fails, which we verify empirically. MCS offers a theoretically grounded and empirically effective alternative to Euclidean cosine similarity for comparing linear probes.

</details>


### [111] [Latent Confounded Causal Discovery via Lie Bracket Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19610)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于范畴论中Kan-Do-Calculus（KDC）的几何与信息论结论，提出了两种在潜在混杂下进行因果发现的算法：BRIDGE和SKFM。前者通过计算干预密度之间的Lie括号残差（Frobenius残差）来识别潜在混淆结构，后者则通过谱方法学习干预流并分解潜在曲率。实验表明两种算法能大幅缩减因果图搜索空间，有效发现含潜在混淆因子的因果模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法在处理潜在混淆时面临指数级搜索空间难题，KDC理论揭示了干预与条件化之间的范畴伴随关系，但其几何和信息论后果尚未被用于算法设计。本文旨在将KDC的几何结构（如Lie括号闭合性失败）转化为可计算的因果发现工具，从而在潜在混淆下高效推断因果结构。

Method: 1) BRIDGE算法：利用干预密度间的Radon-Nikodym导数构造局部因果向量场，通过计算向量场在Lie括号下的闭合失败（Frobenius残差）识别不可积结构，输出候选项给下游评分或可微搜索算法。2) SKFM算法：通过谱方法谱分解潜在曲率张量，学习干预向量场的端到端匹配，直接暴露BRIDGE所指的Lie空间终点。两者均基于KDC的范畴论框架。

Result: 实验证明两种算法能在潜在混杂下发现因果模型，并将可能DAG的超指数搜索空间压缩多个数量级。BRIDGE通过几何筛选实现了高召回率的候选箭线集，SKFM通过谱流匹配进一步精化了干预场的学习。

Conclusion: 本文开创了因果发现的新范式：从干预诱导流的几何结构直接推断潜在结构。KDC的范畴论框架不仅提供了理论边界，还能落地为高效算法，在潜在混淆场景下显著降低搜索复杂度。

Abstract: Recent work on Kan-Do-Calculus (KDC) has established that the boundary between passive observation and active intervention in causal inference is a category-theoretic bi-adjunction, with interventions modeled by left Kan extensions and conditioning by right Kan extensions. This paper introduces two causal discovery algorithms under latent confounding, building on the information-geometric and categorical consequences of KDC. In smooth statistical settings, Radon-Nikodym derivatives between observational and interventional measures induce local causal vector fields; failures of these fields to close under Lie brackets become computable Frobenius residuals, which we interpret as witnesses of failed visible integrability and possible latent or unmodeled structure. Our first algorithm, BRIDGE (Bracket Residuals for Interventional Discovery and Geometric Estimation), combines an interventional density or Radon-Nikodym-ratio engine with a geometric screen that proposes a high-recall family of admissible arrows, identifies non-closing visible pairs as latent-obstruction candidates, and passes the reduced family to downstream score-based or differentiable discovery routines. The second algorithmic contribution, Spectral Kan-Do Flow Matching (SKFM), learns amortized intervention fields and factors latent curvature spectrally, exposing the direct Lie-space endpoint toward which BRIDGE points. A detailed set of experiments show that both algorithms are capable of discovering causal models with latent confounders while collapsing the super-exponential space of possible DAGs by many orders of magnitude. This paper introduces a new paradigm in causal discovery, where latent structure is inferred directly from the geometry of intervention-induced flows.

</details>


### [112] [SEAGAN: domain-Specific and Edge-Aware Graph Attention Network for Dynamic Plant Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19623)
*Antriksh Srivastava,Soumyashree Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将植物生理学中的A-Ci曲线（CO2同化速率与胞间CO2浓度关系曲线）建模为图结构，通过图神经网络对曲线节点进行生化限制状态分类。最佳模型SEAGAN（领域特定与边感知图注意力网络）采用k近邻图结构、边属性编码和图注意力机制，在合成数据集上达到F1-score 0.857和准确率0.882，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物A-Ci曲线中不同数据点对应不同的生化限制状态（如Rubisco限制、RuBP再生限制等），准确识别这些状态是估算光合参数的关键。然而，传统方法依赖人工干预且存在较大不确定性，急需自动化、高精度的分类方法。

Method: 将A-Ci曲线中的每个测量点视为图节点，构建两类图结构：基于距离的k近邻（kNN）图和辅助信号引导（ASG）图，边属性编码点间关系。提出SEAGAN模型，集成领域感知节点特征、边属性、kNN连通性、图注意力机制和加权交叉熵损失，进行节点分类。

Result: 图模型在合成数据集上分类效果优于传统学习基线和自动化拟合基准，尤其在生化过渡区域提升显著。SEAGAN模型F1-score达0.857，准确率0.882。

Conclusion: 将A-Ci曲线表示为图结构能够有效改进生化限制状态分析，其中基于局部k近邻的边感知图注意力机制是最有效的策略，为植物生理数据自动化分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) provide a flexible framework for learning from scientific data linked through physical, biological, or functional relationships. One promising domain is plant physiology, where measured responses often arise from multiple interacting processes whose exact separation remains difficult even with manual intervention. In plant physiology, a key example is the A-Ci curve, which relates net CO2 assimilation rate (Anet) to leaf intercellular CO2 concentration (Ci) and is used to estimate photosynthetic parameters in leaf and crop-canopy models. However, reliable estimation requires identifying the active biochemical limitation state at each curve point, which remains a major source of uncertainty. Here, we formulate limitation-state identification along A-Ci curves as a graph-based node classification problem, with curve points as nodes. Domain-specific graph representations are created using distance-based k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) and auxiliary-signal-guided (ASG) connectivity, with edge attributes encoding pairwise relations. The framework was evaluated against conventional learning baselines, graph-based architectures, and an automated fitting-based benchmark. Results on a large synthetic dataset with known ground-truth limitation states show that graph-based models improve classification, particularly near biochemical transition regions. The best-performing configuration, SEAGAN (domain-Specific and Edge-Aware Graph Attention Network for Dynamic Plant Processes), integrates process-aware node features, edge attributes, kNN connectivity, and graph attention with weighted cross-entropy loss, achieving an F1-score of 0.857 and an accuracy of 0.882. The results show that representing A-Ci curves as graphs improves biochemical limitation-state analysis, with edge-aware attention over local kNN neighborhoods providing the most effective strategy.

</details>


### [113] [MassSpecGym in the Wild: Uncovering and Correcting Evaluation Pitfalls in AI-Driven Molecule Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19624)
*Hongxuan Liu,Roman Bushuiev,Ivy Lightheart,Mrunali Manjrekar,Anton Bushuiev,Magdalena Lederbauer,Filip Jozefov,Yinkai Wang,Soha Hassoun,Josef Sivic,James Taylor,Runzhong Wang,David Healey,Tomáš Pluskal,Connor W. Coley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对基于串联质谱（MS/MS）的分子发现机器学习模型评估进行系统性审计，发现MassSpecGym基准测试中至少17/26篇论文存在评估缺陷，包括数据泄露、捷径学习和实现缺陷三大类问题，并提出改进方案MassSpecGym v1.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的基准测试对开发MS/MS分子发现的机器学习模型至关重要，但实验设计和评估流程中的细微问题会破坏基准可信度、导致错误结论。本文旨在系统审查和量化这些问题。

Method: 以MassSpecGym基准测试套件为案例，全面审查26篇采用该基准的论文，通过大量实验和代码复现，识别并量化数据泄露、捷径学习、实现缺陷和指标偏差三类评估失败模式。

Result: 发现至少17篇论文存在评估缺陷，通过实验量化了这些问题对评价标准的破坏程度，并发布了修复后的MassSpecGym v1.5。

Conclusion: 提炼出适用于MS/MS挑战、基准测试和自定义评估设置的一般性建议，并开源改进版基准测试套件以解决审计中识别的失败模式。

Abstract: Reliable benchmarking is critical for developing machine learning models for tandem mass spectrometry (MS/MS) based molecule discovery. Subtle issues in experimental design and model evaluation procedures can degrade the trustworthiness of such benchmarks and lead to erroneous conclusions. We conduct a thorough review of model evaluation issues in the recent MS/MS machine learning literature, using the standard MassSpecGym benchmark suite as a case study to illustrate the impact of these issues. We find evaluation issues in at least 17 of 26 papers reporting MassSpecGym benchmark results in the first year of its adoption. We isolate three classes of failures: (i) data leakage, (ii) shortcut learning, and (iii) implementation bugs and metric divergence. Through extensive experimentation and code replication, we quantify the impact of these issues and show how they corrupt the evaluation standards MassSpecGym was designed to enforce. We distill our findings into recommendations generalizable to MS/MS challenges, benchmarks, and custom evaluation setups. We also release MassSpecGym v1.5, an implementation of our recommendations in the MassSpecGym benchmarking suite which addresses the failure modes identified in this audit. MassSpecGym v1.5 is publicly available at https://github.com/pluskal-lab/MassSpecGym.

</details>


### [114] [Hard or Just Unreached? Diagnosing the Sampling Blind Spot in Math-Reasoning Difficulty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19636)
*Luca Zhou,Sajel Shah,Emanuele Rodolà,Roberto Dessì*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，数学推理基准中常用的pass@k指标在 hardest 难度层级存在盲区：对于任意采样种子在6次内都无法解决的示例，通过激活嫁接（activation grafting）的确定性解码方法可以在相同计算量下解决其中10.3-22.9%的问题。该方法通过贪婪解码加五种残差流扰动实现，而单独贪婪解码最多只能解决6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示pass@k作为数学推理基准中核心难度信号存在系统性盲区，特别是在最难样本上低估了模型能力。

Method: 采用激活嫁接（activation grafting）对内部表示进行干预，通过贪婪解码加上五种廉价残差流扰动，在确定性推理框架下生成六条链，与原始采样方法进行对比。

Result: 在GSM8K和MATH数据集上，四种开源模型中有10.3-22.9%的pass@k=0%示例可通过激活嫁接方法解决；跨所有12个测试单元验证了扰动机制的不同性（Jaccard <= 0.47）。

Conclusion: pass@k指标对模型真实能力存在低估，尤其是最难样本；激活嫁接揭示的问题可通过残差流的结构性干预恢复，而非标准推理路径可达。

Abstract: Math and science reasoning benchmarks rely on pass@k, the fraction of sampled chains that reach gold, as the canonical per-example difficulty signal. The same signal drives RL with verifiable rewards, math data curation, synthetic curricula, and verifier training. We show this proxy has a persistent blind spot on its hardest stratum: on the eight free-form math cells we test (GSM8K and MATH across four open-weight models), 10.3-22.9% of the examples that no sampling seed solves in six tries are instead solved at matched compute by a six-chain deterministic regime. These are greedy decoding plus five cheap residual-stream perturbations applied via activation grafting, while greedy alone solves at most 6% on these math cells. Recovery scales with the additional budget, across perturbations whose mechanistic distinctness we verify across all twelve cells (cross-kind fix-set Jaccard <= 0.47 in every setup). Activation grafting is used as an intervention on internal representations, not a decoding method; we use it purely as a diagnostic and diversification tool, and our recovered items show that the pass@k= 0 % stratum is structurally identifiable in the residual stream rather than that the unmodified model reaches them under ordinary inference.

</details>


### [115] [Convex training of Lipschitz-regularized shallow neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19652)
*Chao Yin,Antoine Lesage-Landry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对浅层神经网络的训练流程，通过引入可高效全局求解的凸松弛来提升对抗鲁棒性，该凸程序可作为后处理步骤，保证优化后的网络不劣于初始预训练网络。实验表明，该方法在回归任务的对抗设置下，相比现有方法能获得更低的Lipschitz正则化目标函数值，并在某些数据集上同时提升了准确性和对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浅层神经网络训练方法在对抗攻击下鲁棒性不足，而Lipschitz正则化训练程序是非凸的，难以高效求解全局最优解。

Method: 提出一个非凸的Lipschitz正则化训练程序，然后引入一个凸松弛（convex restriction）使其可高效求解全局最优解；该方法可作为后处理步骤，以预训练网络为初始解，保证解不劣于初始值。

Result: 在真实数据集的回归任务对抗设置下，所提凸程序相比现有方法获得更低的Lipschitz正则化目标值，且在部分数据集上同时提升了准确性和对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的凸训练程序能有效提升浅层神经网络在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，并在保持或提升准确性的同时实现更优的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a training procedure for shallow neural networks that promotes robustness against adversarial attacks. We solve a non-convex Lipschitz-regularized training program by introducing a convex restriction that can be efficiently solved to global optimality. Our approach can be employed as a post-processing step by taking a pre-trained network as an initial solution to then solving the convex program whose optimal network is guaranteed to be no worse than the initial one. We illustrate the improvements of our training procedure with experiments using real world datasets for regression tasks under an adversarial setting. We show numerically that solving our proposed convex program yields networks with lower objective values on the Lipschitz-regularized program compared to existing methods. Additionally, we show that on certain datasets, networks obtained using our convex training program are both more accurate and robust with respect to adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [116] [LOKI: Memory-Free Null-Space Constrained Lifelong Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19679)
*Masih Eskandar,Miquel Sirera Perelló,Stratis Ioannidis,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOKI是一种基于希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则的动态层选择和零空间梯度投影方法，用于终身知识编辑，在不访问先前知识的情况下实现高效顺序更新语言模型，平均准确率提升高达14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身知识编辑方法存在两个关键问题：1）对所有新知识样本使用固定层集合进行修改，缺乏灵活性且加剧灾难性遗忘；2）需要访问先前知识和大量预处理来获取数据统计信息。作者旨在解决这两个挑战。

Method: 提出LOKI方法，包含两个核心组件：1）基于希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（HSIC）的动态层选择机制，根据每个新知识样本自适应选择要修改的层；2）将梯度更新投影到模型权重的零空间上，从而避免需要访问先前知识。

Result: 在大量实验上，LOKI相比现有方法取得了优越性能，平均准确率提升高达14%。

Conclusion: LOKI通过动态层选择和零空间投影，有效解决了终身知识编辑中的灵活性和灾难性遗忘问题，同时消除了对先前知识的访问需求，显著提升了编辑性能。

Abstract: Lifelong knowledge editing aims to efficiently and sequentially update language models over time, as new knowledge becomes available or when the model makes mistakes, while preserving acceptable performance on past knowledge. One unresolved challenge is that existing methods modify a fixed set of layers for all new knowledge samples, reducing flexibility and increasing catastrophic forgetting. Another is requiring access to previous knowledge and extensive pre-processing to obtain data statistics. To address these challenges, we introduce LOKI, a novel approach that uses dynamic layer selection based on the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion and projects gradient updates onto the null-space of the model weights, bypassing the requirement for previous knowledge access. We show that LOKI achieves superior performance to existing approaches across a wide variety of experiments, achieving up to a 14\% improvement in average accuracy.

</details>


### [117] [Multi-Granular Attention-Driven Reinforcement Learning Framework for Web Intelligent Enhancement Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19690)
*Navin Chhibber,Deepak Singh,Anokh Kishore,Nikita Chawla,K. Anguraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多粒度注意力增强的Web智能增强系统（MGAR-WIES），通过语义图建模、注意力机制和自适应强化学习，在异构动态Web数据上实现个性化服务，准确率达80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习、深度学习和强化学习模型在持续演化的Web环境中难以处理异构动态数据，存在语义理解不足、适应性差和可扩展性有限的问题。

Method: 1) 收集并预处理结构化、半结构化和非结构化Web数据生成统一特征表示；2) 构建动态语义图，利用注意力增强的图嵌入捕获局部相关性和全局上下文依赖；3) 采用自适应多智能体强化学习策略，基于注意力感知的语义状态优化个性化Web动作；4) 集成连续在线反馈实时更新图表示和学习策略。

Result: 提出的MGAR-WIES在准确率（80%）上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MGAR-WIES通过融合语义图建模、注意力机制和自适应强化学习，有效解决了Web智能增强系统中的语义理解、适应性和可扩展性挑战。

Abstract: From the past few years, web intelligent enhancement systems increasingly rely on heterogeneous and dynamic web data to deliver personalized, context-aware services. However, traditional machine learning, deep learning, and reinforcement learning models often struggle with semantic understanding, adaptability, and scalability in continuously evolving web environments. In this research, a Multi-Granular Attention-based Reinforcement Web Intelligent Enhancement System (MGAR-WIES) is proposed to address the challenges by integrating semantic graph modeling, attention mechanisms, and adaptive reinforcement learning. Initially, heterogeneous web data comprising structured, semi-structured and unstructured sources are collected and preprocessed for generating unified feature representations. These representations are transformed into a dynamic semantic graph, where entities and their relationships are modeled by using graph embeddings enhanced by attention mechanisms for capturing both local relevance and global contextual dependencies. Subsequently, an adaptive multi-agent reinforcement learning strategy leverages the attention-aware semantic states to optimize personalized web actions like content recommendation, navigation optimization, and service adaptation. Finally, the continuous online feedback is further integrated to update graph representations and learning policies in real time by ensuring sustained adaptability and performance. The proposed MGAR-WIES acheived better results in terms of accuracy (80%) when compared with existing approaches.

</details>


### [118] [Efficiently Representing Algorithms With Chain-of-Thought Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19697)
*Yanhong Li,Anej Svete,Ashish Sabharwal,William Merrill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，链式思维（CoT）Transformer 可以高效模拟 Word RAM 算法，仅需多对数开销，远优于模拟图灵机所需的二次开销。该结论在有限精度、连续CoT和循环混合架构下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论表明CoT Transformer可以模拟图灵机，但图灵机不便于描述和分析实际算法；Word RAM模型更贴近真实算法设计。因此自然提出：CoT Transformer能否高效模拟Word RAM算法？

Method: 首先证明有限精度、多对数宽度、右最右唯一硬注意力Transformer能以多对数开销模拟Word RAM；然后将结果推广到两种更实用的设置：连续CoT（推理以向量而非token形式呈现）和混合架构（Transformer层叠在线性RNN之上）。

Result: CoT Transformer可以高效模拟任何Word RAM算法，仅需多对数开销。对于"扁平"指令集的Word RAM，开销降至对数平方；对于无乘法扁平指令集，开销仅为对数级。这显著优于模拟图灵机所需的二次开销。

Conclusion: CoT Transformer不仅能模拟图灵机以完成任意计算，还能以远高于图灵机的效率模拟更贴近实际算法的Word RAM模型，为推理模型的实用性提供了更强的理论支撑。

Abstract: The increasing popularity of \emph{reasoning} models -- language models that output a series of reasoning or thought tokens before producing an answer -- is justified, in part, by theoretical results showing that chain-of-thought (CoT) transformers can simulate Turing machines, and thus perform arbitrary computation. However, the Turing machine, while suitable for complexity-theoretic analysis, is not convenient, intuitive, or efficient for discussing algorithms. Algorithms are typically designed and analyzed at a higher level of abstraction, captured by the \emph{Word RAM} model with random-access memory and unit-cost operations on $\bigO(\log n)$-bit words. As a result, Word RAM algorithms can be substantially more efficient than their Turing machine counterparts, raising the question: \emph{Can CoT transformers efficiently simulate Word RAM algorithms?} For instance, can they sort $n$ items in $\bigO(n \log n)$ steps or run Dijkstra's algorithm in $\bigO(E + V \log V)$ steps? We answer affirmatively, up to poly-logarithmic overhead. We first establish this for finite-precision transformers with poly-logarithmic width and rightmost unique hard attention, then strengthen the result to two more practical settings with finite width and log-precision: \emph{continuous} CoT, where reasoning takes the form of vectors rather than tokens, and a \emph{hybrid} architecture in which transformer layers sit atop a recurrent (linear RNN) layer. In all three cases, we find that CoT \emph{can} efficiently simulate any Word RAM algorithm with only a poly-logarithmic overhead in $n$. This overhead reduces to log-square when the Word RAM has a ``flat'' instruction set, and only logarithmic for multiplication-free flat instructions -- in stark contrast to known CoT simulations of Turing machines, which require quadratic overhead over Word RAM.

</details>


### [119] [Efficient Neural Network Model Selection for Few-Class Application Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19712)
*Bryan Bo Cao,Abhinav Sharma,Lawrence O'Gorman,Michael Coss,Shubham Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对少类别（通常少于十类）数据集的模型选择问题，提出了一种基于数据属性的分类难度度量方法，能够在6到29倍的速度下完成模型与数据集的比较，并扩展了模型家族至更小规模，在移动机器人、无人机和物联网等资源受限场景中实现了显著效率提升（如模型比YOLOv5-nano小42%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注高性能神经网络的开发与基准测试，忽视了数据集属性（尤其是类别数量少这一常见但未被充分研究的情况）对高效模型选择的指导作用。传统模型选择方法在少类别数据集上效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种基于数据侧属性的分类难度度量指标，利用"少类别区分性"现象，使模型和数据集比较速度比重复训练测试快6-29倍；将缩放模型家族扩展到已发布的最小模型以下。

Result: 模型选择速度快6-29倍；在移动机器人任务上，模型比YOLOv5-nano小42%但达到相似精度；在移动机器人、无人机和物联网场景中验证了实际效率提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的分类难度度量能够显著加速少类别数据集的模型选择过程，并通过扩展更小模型在资源受限应用中实现精度与效率的更好平衡。

Abstract: While much effort has focused on developing and benchmarking high-performance neural networks, less attention has been given to how dataset properties, known to practitioners, can guide efficient model selection. Neural models are typically evaluated on datasets with thousands of classes, yet many real-world applications involve fewer than ten. To address this understudied but common setting, we develop a measure of classification difficulty based on data-side properties and show how it enables more efficient model selection for few-class datasets, where traditional approaches are less effective. We term this phenomenon "few-class distinctiveness". Our metric allows comparison of models and datasets 6 to 29$\times$ faster than repeated training and testing. Leveraging this insight, we extend scaled model families below the smallest published models, achieving greater efficiency at similar accuracy, for example models up to 42% smaller than YOLOv5-nano for a mobile robot task. Targeting resource-constrained applications, we demonstrate few-class model selection across mobile robot, drone, and IoT scenarios, highlighting practical gains in efficiency without sacrificing performance.

</details>


### [120] [OnDeFog: Online Decision Transformer under Frame Dropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19721)
*Daiki Yotsufuji,Kenta Nishihara,Shoma Shimizu,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OnDeFog方法，将DeFog的抗随机帧丢弃机制与在线决策Transformer（ODT）结合，以解决离线方法DeFog无法有效泛化到未见状态的问题。实验表明，OnDeFog在高帧丢弃率环境下优于ODT，且在含大量低奖励数据的数据集上优于DeFog。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFog虽然能缓解帧丢弃导致的性能下降，但作为离线学习方法，难以有效泛化到训练数据中未充分覆盖的新状态，限制了其在真实世界强化学习应用中的适用性。

Method: 将DeFog中处理随机帧丢弃的机制集成到在线决策Transformer（ODT）中，使得模型能够通过与环境直接交互在线学习策略，同时保持对帧丢弃的鲁棒性。

Result: 在高帧丢弃率环境中，OnDeFog性能优于ODT；在包含大量低奖励数据的数据集上，OnDeFog性能优于DeFog。

Conclusion: 将帧丢弃处理机制与在线强化学习相结合可以有效缓解离线方法的泛化不足问题，OnDeFog在帧丢弃场景下兼具鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: In challenging real-world reinforcement learning applications, communication delays or sensor failures often cause frame dropping, in which the agent cannot receive the dropped states and associated rewards. To address the performance degradation caused by frame dropping, the Decision Transformer under Random Frame Dropping (DeFog) was developed by incorporating additional mechanisms into the decision transformer to tackle frame dropping. Although DeFog can mitigate performance degradation in frame-dropping environments, since DeFog is an offline learning method, it struggles to effectively generalize to novel states not adequately represented in the training dataset. In this study, we propose OnDeFog, which integrates the mechanisms in DeFog with the online decision transformer (ODT), an online reinforcement learning method that learns policies through direct environmental interaction. Comprehensive experimental evaluation demonstrates that our proposed OnDeFog achieves superior performance compared to ODT in environments characterized by high dropping frame rate and outperforms DeFog on datasets containing a large amount of low-reward data.

</details>


### [121] [Federated Bilevel Performative Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19734)
*Liangxin Qian,Chang Liu,Xuanyu Cao,Jun Zhao,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦双层执行性预测问题，其中上下层目标函数均依赖于客户端的决策依赖分布。提出了联邦双层执行性稳定点（FBPS）的概念，并开发了两种求解方法：线性收敛的FBi-RRM和通信高效的FBi-SGD。实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦双层优化方法假设客户端数据分布固定，但实际中模型部署会改变用户行为和数据分布（执行性效应），导致分布偏移。本文旨在解决这一现实挑战。

Method: 形式化定义了联邦双层执行性稳定点（FBPS），提出解耦风险视角并给出存在唯一性的充分条件。开发了两种算法：FBi-RRM（基于响应重匹配，满足收缩条件时线性收敛）和FBi-SGD（基于联邦超梯度估计的通信高效随机方法，在步长衰减下保证收敛）。

Result: 在策略回归和元策略分类任务上验证了理论预测的稳定性阈值，相比非执行性基线提升了元泛化性能。CNN实验进一步证明了在非凸神经网络场景下的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 提出并求解了考虑执行性效应的联邦双层优化问题，理论上证明了FBPS点的存在唯一性条件，实验上验证了所提方法在各类任务中的有效性和泛化优势。

Abstract: Federated bilevel optimization is widely used for nested learning problems across distributed clients, such as federated hyperparameter tuning and meta-learning under privacy and communication constraints. Most existing formulations assume fixed client data distributions, which can be violated by performativity, where deployed decisions reshape client behavior and data collection, inducing client-specific, decision-dependent distribution shift. We study federated bilevel performative prediction, where both upper-level (UL) and lower-level (LL) objectives are evaluated under client-dependent, decision-dependent distributions. We formalize the federated bilevel performatively stable (FBPS) point under a decoupled-risk perspective and provide sufficient conditions for its existence and uniqueness. We then develop two federated methods to compute the FBPS solution: FBi-RRM, which converges linearly under a contraction condition, and FBi-SGD, a communication-efficient stochastic method based on federated hypergradient estimation with convergence guarantees under diminishing step sizes when sensitivities are sufficiently small. Experiments on strategic regression and meta strategic classification validate the predicted stability thresholds and demonstrate improved meta-generalization over non-performative baselines, and CNN-based classification further demonstrates the practical effectiveness of the proposed methods in nonconvex neural network settings.

</details>


### [122] [Manifold Bandits: Bayesian Curriculum Learning over the Latent Geometry of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19750)
*Darrien McKenzie,Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯流形课程（BMC）框架，将问题采样建模为具有内生非平稳性的流形结构赌博机问题，通过构建层次化任务树并应用贝叶斯学习来指导采样，实验表明仅优先难度不足以实现强下游性能，需考虑结构性和类型感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应课程学习方法通常将问题选择视为独立臂的标准赌博机问题，忽略任务空间的结构化异质性，导致训练效率受限。

Method: 将问题采样建模为流形结构赌博机问题，引入贝叶斯流形课程（BMC）框架，通过组织层次化任务树并应用贝叶斯学习来指导采样过程。

Result: 不同采样策略在生产力（学习信号）、多样性（任务流形覆盖）和效用（评估相关性）之间存在非平凡权衡，仅优先难度不足以获得强下游性能。

Conclusion: 问题采样需考虑任务空间的结构性和类型感知，结构感知的课程学习对提升LLM推理能力至关重要。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a central approach for improving reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs), where training efficiency depends critically on how problems are sampled during optimization. Existing adaptive curriculum learning methods typically prioritize prompts of intermediate difficulty, treating problem selection as a standard bandit problem with independent arms and overlooking the structured, heterogeneous nature of the task space. In this work, we frame problem sampling as a manifold-structured bandit problem with endogenous non-stationarity: problems are related through the model's latent representation space, and sampling decisions can steer how learning signals evolve across that space. To operationalize this perspective, we introduce Bayesian Manifold Curriculum (BMC), a structure-aware framework that organizes problems into a hierarchical task tree and applies Bayesian learning to guide sampling. Empirically, we find that different sampling strategies induce non-trivial tradeoffs between productivity (learning signal), diversity (coverage of the task manifold), and utility (evaluation relevance). These results show that prioritizing difficulty alone is insufficient for strong downstream performance, highlighting the importance of incorporating structure and type-awareness into problem sampling.

</details>


### [123] [Learning universal approximations for partial differential equations with Physics-Informed Broad Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19754)
*Zhiwen Yu,Derong Yang,Liujian Zhang,Kaixiang Yang,Peilin Zhan,Jianmin Lv,Jane You,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息宽度学习系统（PIBLS），一种无需反向传播的PDE求解框架，通过直接最小二乘优化将求解速度提升1-3个数量级，同时保持高精度，并给出非线性PDE的通用逼近性理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器计算成本高（网格依赖），而PINNs收敛慢且优化不稳定。旨在弥合两者差距，提出一种高效、无需反向传播的PDE求解框架。

Method: 提出PIBLS框架，将PDE求解转化为直接最小二乘优化（无需反向传播）；针对非线性PDE改进算法；给出通用逼近性质的严格数学证明。

Result: 在線性和非线性PDE上，PIBLS比传统PINNs快1-3个数量级，同时解精度显著更高。

Conclusion: PIBLS为科学机器学习提供了一种计算高效的新范式，适用于实时仿真和设计优化等需要高速计算的任务。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) play a central role in modeling complex physical, biological, and engineering systems. While traditional numerical solvers are robust, they often incur prohibitive computational costs due to mesh dependencies, whereas recent Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) offer a mesh-free alternative but frequently suffer from slow convergence and optimization instability. To bridge this gap, this article proposes the Physics-Informed Broad Learning System (PIBLS), a novel backpropagation-free framework that reformulates PDE solving as a direct least-squares optimization. We improved an algorithm within this framework to handle nonlinear PDEs efficiently and provide a rigorous mathematical proof establishing the universal approximation property of PIBLS for these equations. Experiments on linear and nonlinear PDEs demonstrate that PIBLS is one to three orders of magnitude faster than conventional PINNs while achieving significantly higher solution accuracy. This framework provides a computationally efficient paradigm for scientific machine learning, offering a practical, high-speed alternative for real-time simulation and design optimization tasks.

</details>


### [124] [An Information Theoretic Framework for Graph Novelty Generation via Latent Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19770)
*Itsuki Nakagawa,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于信息论的图数据新颖性生成框架，通过描述长度约束确保生成数据既偏离已有模式又保持全局结构一致，理论证明误分类概率收敛于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图生成方法缺乏对"新颖性"的量化定义和可控生成机制，难以生成既新颖又结构可靠的数据。

Method: 将数据嵌入潜空间，用有限混合模型建模潜分布；基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则，要求生成样本难以被现有混合分量解释（新颖性），同时限制其对整体混合结构的影响（可靠性）。

Result: 理论分析表明选择合适的阈值后，非新颖或不可靠样本的误分类概率以显式速率收敛到零；合成数据和基准图数据集实验验证了方法能实现可量化风险的新颖性生成。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了首个可量化风险、兼顾全局一致性的图数据新颖性生成方案。

Abstract: We propose an information-theoretic framework for graph novelty generation, which aims to generate data that are distinct from existing patterns while preserving global structural consistency. Our approach embeds data into a latent space, models the latent distribution using finite mixture models, and generates novel samples by imposing explicit novelty and reliability conditions formulated in terms of description length. Specifically, novelty is enforced by requiring generated samples to be poorly explained by all existing mixture components, while reliability constrains their impact on the overall mixture structure under the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that, with appropriate threshold choices, the probabilities of misclassifying non-novel or unreliable samples converge to zero with explicit rates. Experiments on synthetic and benchmark graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed method enables principled novelty generation with quantifiable risk.

</details>


### [125] [Flow Map Denoisers: Traversing the Distortion-Perception Plane for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19802)
*Nicolas Zilberstein,Morteza Mardani,Santiago Segarra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现流映射模型（flow map models）隐式定义了一个单参数族去噪器，通过改变前瞻参数t可以连续地在失真-感知（DP）前沿上移动，从MMSE区域到感知区域，无需额外训练或调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像复原面临失真与感知质量的根本权衡：现有方法要么固定于DP前沿的单一点，要么需要成对数据监督、辅助模型或超参数调优才能访问不同点。本文旨在寻找一种无需这些额外开销即可连续调控DP权衡的方法。

Method: 利用流映射模型（flow matching的扩展，用于少步采样）学习平均场，其前瞻参数t作为控制旋钮，隐式定义了一个单参数族去噪器，连续跨越DP前沿。对于高斯目标证明其理论上可精确恢复最优DP前沿；对于自然图像通过实验验证。该机制通过Plug-and-Play求解器扩展到一般逆问题。

Result: 在CelebA (128×128)和AFHQ (256×256)上的多个线性和非线性逆任务中，单一训练的流映射模型即可覆盖DP权衡，在两个极端上匹配或超越专门的基线方法。

Conclusion: 流映射模型的lookahead参数t提供了一种简洁、无需额外训练或调参的方式，在图像复原中连续地控制失真-感知权衡，并在一般逆问题中实现感知对齐与数据一致性之间的调节。

Abstract: Image restoration faces a fundamental tradeoff: methods that minimize error produce blurry reconstructions, while those that maximize perceptual quality yield sharp but less faithful images. Existing approaches either commit to a single operating point on this distortion perception (DP) frontier or require paired-data supervision, auxiliary models, or hyperparameter tuning of the sampler to access different points. We show that flow map models, a recent extension of flow matching for few-step sampling that learns an average field, implicitly define a one-parameter family of denoisers that continuously spans the DP frontier. The lookahead parameter t acts as a control knob between the MMSE and perceptual regimes. For Gaussian targets, we prove that varying t exactly recovers the optimal DP frontier; for natural images, we observe similar behavior empirically. Within a Plug-and-Play solver, the same mechanism extends to general inverse problems, where it controls a tradeoff between perceptual alignment and data consistency. Despite the lack of exact optimality guarantees in this setting, a single trained flow map spans the DP tradeoff, matching or exceeding specialized baselines at both extremes. Extensive experiments on CelebA ($128\times 128$) and AFHQ ($256\times 256$) across several linear and nonlinear inverse tasks validate our findings.

</details>


### [126] [Uncertainty-Aware Reward Modeling for Stable RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19818)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yichen Sun,Yunsheng Lu,Shijian Wang,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Zhixuan Chu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知奖励建模（UARM），通过分位数保形预测校准奖励模型的不确定性，并利用异方差方差分解重新加权GRPO优势，从而减轻奖励破解问题，提升对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中奖励模型作为确定性点估计无法指示预测不可靠性，且基于分组的策略优化（如GRPO）会统一处理奖励，当策略探索多样化响应时，不可靠的奖励估计可能被赋予过大影响力，引发严重的奖励破解问题。

Method: 提出UARM：1) 基于分位数保形预测为奖励模型提供校准的不确定性估计；2) 通过异方差方差分解重新加权GRPO中的优势函数，降低不可靠奖励的影响。

Result: 在HelpSteer、UltraFeedback和PKU-SafeRLHF三个基准上，UARM显著改善了奖励模型校准质量、减少了奖励破解现象，并提升了下游对齐性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的奖励建模能够有效缓解RLHF中由不可靠奖励估计引发的奖励破解问题，是一种优于标准GRPO和忽略不确定性基线的方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) aligns large language models by training reward models on preference data and optimizing policies to maximize predicted rewards. However, this pipeline faces two fundamental challenges: (1) reward models cannot signal when their predictions are unreliable, since they usually act as deterministic point estimators; and (2) modern group-based policy optimization can amplify unreliable reward signals, as exemplified by GRPO's uniform treatment of rewards during advantage computation. As policies explore increasingly diverse responses, these two limitations create a critical vulnerability: unreliable reward estimates may be granted disproportionate influence, triggering severe reward hacking. We propose Uncertainty-Aware Reward Modeling (UARM), which equips reward models with calibrated uncertainty via quantile-based conformal prediction and reweights GRPO advantages through heteroscedastic variance decomposition. Experiments across HelpSteer, UltraFeedback, and PKU-SafeRLHF demonstrate that UARM significantly improves reward model calibration, reduces reward hacking, and enhances downstream alignment quality compared to standard GRPO and uncertainty-agnostic baselines.

</details>


### [127] [Enhancing Graph Neural Networks Using Proximity Graphs for Dust Source Emission Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19825)
*Maryam Sanisales,Zahed Rahmati,Ali Darvishi Boloorani,Ali Vefghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用邻近图（Delaunay三角剖分、Gabriel图、k近邻图、Yao图）作为图神经网络（GraphSAGE、GCN、GAT）的输入进行消息传递，实现尘源排放的精准预测。实验表明，基于邻近图的GNN方法显著优于随机图GNN和LSTM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尘暴对环境和健康造成严重危害，传统预测方法难以捕捉尘源排放复杂的时空动态，亟需更有效的建模方法。

Method: 采用多种邻近图（Delaunay三角剖分、Gabriel图、k-NN图、Yao图）作为图神经网络（GraphSAGE、GCN、GAT）的输入结构，通过消息传递机制建模数据点间的时空关系。

Result: 基于邻近图的GNN方法在尘源排放预测中显著优于随机图GNN和LSTM模型。

Conclusion: 邻近图与图神经网络的集成能有效提升尘源预测的鲁棒性和准确性，证明了图结构表示在该任务中的关键作用。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of dust source emissions is critical for mitigating the significant environmental and health hazards posed by dust storms. Traditional forecasting methods often struggle to capture the complex spatiotemporal dynamics of these phenomena.
  In this paper, we demonstrate that proximity graphs enable Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to effectively model the intricate spatial and temporal relationships between data points. Specifically, we use proximity graphs--such as Delaunay triangulation, Gabriel graph, k-Nearest Neighbor graph, and Yao graph--as the input for GNNs (including GraphSAGE, Graph Convolutional Networks, and Graph Attention Networks) to perform message passing.
  Our approach highlights the effectiveness of integrating proximity graphs with GNNs for robust and accurate dust source forecasting. To emphasize the importance of proximity graph representations, we compare our method against GNNs using random graphs for message passing. The results show that GNNs with proximity graphs significantly outperform those with random graphs and are also far superior to Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) model in dust source emission forecasting.

</details>


### [128] [When, Where, and How: Adaptive Binning for Tabular Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19827)
*Daehwan Kim,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Adaptive Binning的自适应分箱预文本方法，用于医疗表格数据的自监督学习。该方法通过特征级别的粗到细课程学习策略，逐步细化分箱，并利用异构感知目标统一处理类别和数值特征。在多个公开医疗表格数据集上，该方法在线性探测和微调中均取得一致提升，并建立了标准化的医疗表格SSL基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗表格数据在临床研究中广泛存在，但深度学习方法在表格数据上的应用受到限制，主要因为可靠标签通常需要昂贵的人工裁决。虽然自监督学习可以利用无标签表格数据，但现有的基于分箱的预文本方法使用固定的全局分位数离散化和特征无关的监督信号，未能充分利用特征特性。

Method: 提出Adaptive Binning方法：(1) 训练自适应的分箱离散化预文本，将离散化与学习过程耦合；(2) 采用特征级别的粗到细课程学习，在检测到性能平台期后逐步细化每个特征的分箱；(3) 选择表征感知的分裂点，同时改善值空间集中度和表征空间一致性；(4) 设计异构感知目标，统一处理类别特征的分类重建和数值特征的序数监督。

Result: 在多个公开医疗表格数据集上，使用统一的评估协议进行实验，Adaptive Binning在线性探测和微调任务中均取得一致的性能提升，且无需针对数据集进行离散化调参。

Conclusion: Adaptive Binning通过将离散化与学习过程自适应耦合，有效提升了医疗表格数据自监督学习的性能。同时，该工作还引入了一个标准化的医疗表格SSL基准，有助于推动该领域的可重复研究。

Abstract: Medical tabular data are ubiquitous in clinical research, but deep learning for tables remains underexplored because reliable labels often require costly expert adjudication, even though structured clinical variables are routinely available in tabular form. Self-supervised learning can leverage these unlabeled tables, and recent binning-based pretexts offer a promising inductive bias, but existing objectives fix a single global quantile discretization and apply feature-agnostic supervision. We propose Adaptive Binning, a training-adaptive discretization pretext for tabular SSL that couples discretization to learning through a feature-wise coarse-to-fine curriculum. Motivated by the spectral bias of neural networks and the principles of curriculum learning, our method progressively refines discretization per feature upon plateau detection and selects representation-aware splits to jointly improve value-space concentration and representation-space coherence. A heterogeneity-aware objective unifies categorical reconstruction with ordinal supervision for numerical features, and experiments on public medical tabular datasets under unified evaluation protocols show consistent gains for linear probing and fine-tuning without dataset-specific discretization tuning. We further introduce a medical tabular SSL benchmark with standardized protocols to support reproducible progress in this underexplored domain. Our code is available at https://github.com/labhai/Adaptive-Binning.

</details>


### [129] [Neural Additive and Basis Models with Feature Selection and Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19850)
*Yasutoshi Kishimoto,Kota Yamanishi,Takuya Matsuda,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在神经加性模型（NAM）和神经基础模型（NBM）中引入特征选择层，在训练过程中更新选择权重，以减少计算成本和模型大小，从而有效解决高维数据和特征交互场景下的计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAM和NBM虽具有可解释性优势，但在考虑特征交互（使用双输入神经网络）或处理高维数据集时，计算资源需求急剧增加，导致训练变得棘手。

Method: 在NAM和NBM中引入特征选择层，在训练过程中动态更新特征选择权重，通过筛选重要特征来降低计算复杂度。

Result: 相比原始NAM和NBM，所提方法降低了计算成本和模型大小；即使在处理高维数据集时也能有效使用双输入神经网络捕获特征交互；与最先进的GAMs相比，性能更好或相当。

Conclusion: 特征选择机制是解决NAM/NBM在高维和特征交互场景下计算瓶颈的有效手段，能在保持可解释性的同时提升计算效率。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) exhibit attractive performance in various fields but often suffer from low interpretability. The neural additive model (NAM) and its variant called the neural basis model (NBM) use neural networks (NNs) as nonlinear shape functions in generalized additive models (GAMs). Both models are highly interpretable and exhibit good performance and flexibility for NN training. NAM and NBM can provide and visualize the contribution of each feature to the prediction owing to GAM-based architectures. However, when using two-input NNs to consider feature interactions or when applying them to high-dimensional datasets, training NAM and NBM becomes intractable due to the increase in the computational resources required. This paper proposes incorporating the feature selection mechanism into NAM and NBM to resolve computational bottlenecks. We introduce the feature selection layer in both models and update the selection weights during training. Our method is simple and can reduce computational costs and model sizes compared to vanilla NAM and NBM. In addition, it enables us to use two-input NNs even in high-dimensional datasets and capture feature interactions. We demonstrate that the proposed models are computationally efficient compared to vanilla NAM and NBM, and they exhibit better or comparable performance with state-of-the-art GAMs.

</details>


### [130] [Physics-Informed Neural Network with Squeeze-Excitation-like Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19853)
*Yun-Fei Song,Long-Gang Pang,Fu-Peng Li,Jun-Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEA-PINN通过挤压-激励注意力机制动态校准神经元重要性，实现稳定初始化（17/20基准问题近乎零方差），无需傅里叶特征或周期激活函数即可与高频专用模型TSA-PINN竞争（精度提升83-90%），且作为轻量插件可提升TSA-PINN性能42.49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINN）在训练中常因初始化不稳定、收敛困难而性能不佳，尤其在处理高频问题时表现脆弱。现有方法如傅里叶特征嵌入或周期激活函数虽有所改善但增加了模型复杂度。作者旨在设计一种轻量级、即插即用的模块，通过动态特征重标定提升PINN的非线性表达能力和训练稳定性。

Method: 提出SEA-PINN架构，核心是在PINN中嵌入挤压-激励（Squeeze-Excitation）类注意力机制，对跨层神经元的特征重要性进行动态重校准。此外，设计高度稳定的初始化策略。该方法无需傅里叶特征嵌入或周期激活函数，可作为轻量插件模块集成到现有PINN架构中。

Result: 在20个基准问题中，SEA-PINN在17个上实现近乎可忽略的方差和显著降低的初始损失。在高频案例7上，相比标准FNN-PINN提升83-90%精度，与高频专用模型TSA-PINN竞争力相当。将SEA-PINN集成到TSA-PINN中进一步提升了42.49%的性能。

Conclusion: SEA-PINN作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能有效增强非线性表示能力、促进更稳健高效的收敛，并提升物理信息学习的整体可靠性。其稳定初始化和无需专门架构设计的特性使其具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: We introduce SEA-PINN, a novel architecture that incorporates a Squeeze-Excitation-like attention mechanism into physics-informed neural networks to dynamically recalibrate the importance of neurons across layers. A key feature of SEA-PINN is its highly stable initialization. On 17 out of 20 benchmark problems, SEA-PINN exhibit nearly negligible variance and significantly reduced initial loss, establishing a quasi-deterministic and favorable starting point for optimization. Notably, without employing Fourier feature embeddings or periodic activation functions, SEA-PINN attained competitive accuracy (83\% vs. 90\% improvement relative to FNN-PINN on the high-frequency case 7) as compared with TSA-PINN-a model specifically engineered for high-frequency problems via learnable frequencies in sinusoidal activations. Furthermore, integrating SEA-PINN into TSA-PINN boosted performance by 42.49\%. These results underscore SEA-PINN as a lightweight plug-in module that enhances nonlinear representation power, promotes more robust and efficient convergence, and strengthens the overall reliability of physics-informed learning.

</details>


### [131] [Global Convergence of Gradient Descent for Score Matching in Gaussian Mixtures via Reverse Fisher Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19876)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了反向Fisher散度作为评分匹配的替代目标函数，证明在高斯混合模型场景下，梯度下降从任意初始化出发都能实现全局收敛，且优化景观优于前向Fisher散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的前向Fisher散度（期望对教师分布取）在高斯混合模型等简单设置下会导致不良的、依赖初始化的收敛行为，亟需寻找具有更好优化性质的目标函数。

Method: 采用反向Fisher散度（期望对学生分布取）作为替代目标，使用梯度下降（GD）拟合高斯混合模型，并基于Lyapunov函数分析梯度下降动力学。

Result: 1) 教师为单高斯、学生为固定权重和单位协方差的高斯混合模型时，从任意初始化GD全局收敛；2) 教师也为高斯混合模型时，在全局随机初始化和目标均值˜Ω(1)-分离假设下，每个学生分量高概率收敛到最近的教师分量，学生分布在全变差距离下也收敛。

Conclusion: 反向Fisher散度相比前向Fisher散度具有更优越的优化景观，为评分匹配问题提供了更好的训练目标选择。

Abstract: The score matching problem is a central training objective in modern generative modeling, diffusion models, fitting unnormalized statistical models, and inverse problems. A standard approach is to minimize the forward Fisher divergence, where the expectation is taken with respect to the teacher distribution. However, recent results show that even in simple Gaussian mixture model settings, this objective can lead to undesirable and initialization-dependent convergence behavior. In this paper, we study an alternative objective: the reverse Fisher divergence, where the expectation is taken with respect to the student distribution. We analyze gradient descent (GD) for fitting Gaussian mixture models and show that this change in the objective leads to significantly better optimization properties. First, when the teacher distribution is a single Gaussian and the student is a Gaussian mixture model with fixed weights and identity covariances, we prove the global convergence of GD from arbitrary initializations. Second, we extend the analysis to the case where the teacher is also a Gaussian mixture model and prove global convergence guarantees under a global random initialization scheme and a $\widetildeΩ(1)$-separation assumption on the target means. In particular, with high probability, each student component converges near its closest teacher component, and we provide conditions under which the student distribution converges in total variation distance. Our proofs rely on a new Lyapunov-based analysis of the gradient descent dynamics, showing that the reverse Fisher divergence has a much more favorable optimization landscape than the forward Fisher divergence.

</details>


### [132] [On the Oracle Complexity of Interpolation-Based Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19878)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PPI-GD算法，利用数据域中的分段多项式插值近似梯度，以降低梯度下降的预言机复杂度，在强凸和非凸损失函数上优于多种GD变体，并拓展了多元张量积多项式插值的误差分析技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现，ERM损失函数在训练数据上的光滑性（而非优化参数上的光滑性）可用于改进梯度下降的预言机复杂度，本文旨在利用这一特性设计更高效的优化算法。

Method: 提出PPI-GD（分段多项式插值梯度下降）算法，每次迭代在数据域中等距点查询一阶预言机构造多项式插值，近似完整梯度；数据空间维度为训练样本数的多对数函数时，分析了强凸和非凸损失函数的预言机复杂度。

Result: PPI-GD在损失函数足够光滑的关键区域优于多种GD变体；将双三次样条插值的误差分析技术推广到了d维张量积多项式插值。

Conclusion: PPI-GD利用数据域光滑性有效降低了预言机复杂度，所提出的多元插值误差分析方法具有独立理论价值。

Abstract: Recent work on first-order optimizers for empirical risk minimization (ERM) has suggested that smoothness of ERM loss functions in the training data, rather than in the optimization parameters, can be leveraged to improve the oracle complexity of gradient descent (GD) methods. In this paper, we propose an inexact gradient method, piecewise polynomial interpolation-based gradient descent (PPI-GD), which approximates the full gradient in each iteration by querying the first-order oracle at equidistant points in the data domain to construct polynomial interpolants of the resulting gradient samples over appropriately sized patches of the data domain. We analyze the oracle complexity of PPI-GD for strongly convex and non-convex loss functions when the data space dimension is bounded by a polylogarithmic function of the number of training samples, and find it to outperform several GD variants in key regimes when the loss function is sufficiently smooth. Furthermore, our analysis extends several techniques from the error analysis of bicubic spline interpolants to the setting of $d$-variate tensor product polynomial interpolants which may be of independent interest in interpolation analysis.

</details>


### [133] [Matching Markets meet Cumulative Prospect Theory: Towards Optimal and Adversarially Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19883)
*Ananya Kunisetty,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了竞争性双边匹配市场下的多智能体多臂老虎机问题，采用累积前景理论(CPT)模拟人类偏好，分析了CPT权重扭曲奖励下的学习算法，获得了玩家最优遗憾界，并通过主动选择探索臂集进一步改进了遗憾界。此外，还考虑了对抗性市场中的鲁棒算法，在已知和未知总腐败预算两种情况下均建立了对数级遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将累积前景理论(CPT)引入多智能体多臂老虎机匹配市场问题，以更好地模拟人类决策中的风险偏好和非线性权重感知，弥补传统期望效用理论在描述人类实际行为时的不足。

Method: 采用CPT权重扭曲奖励函数（使用α-Hölder连续权重函数），分析现有算法的遗憾界；通过主动选择探索期间的臂集来消除主导项中对K的依赖，获得改进的遗憾界；针对对抗性市场，设计了在已知和未知腐败预算下的鲁棒算法。

Result: 获得玩家最优遗憾界O(K log T (1/Δ)^{2/α})；改进后的算法在K>>N时消除了K在主导项中的依赖，达到最优遗憾；在对抗性市场中，已知和未知腐败预算下均建立了对数级玩家最优遗憾保证。

Conclusion: CPT框架下竞争性匹配市场的多智能体学习算法具有理论保证，主动臂集选择策略能有效降低维度影响，且算法在对抗性腐败环境下依然保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study a multi-agent multi-armed bandit problem in the competitive setup with two-sided matching markets under a human centric decision making model. To capture human preferences, we use cumulative prospect theory (CPT) that weighs the actions of the agent in a nonlinear fashion using a ($α$-Hölder continuous) weight function. CPT has been widely used in behavioral economics and risk sensitive machine learning to emulate human preferences. We analyze the state-of-the-art learning algorithm with CPT weight distorted rewards and obtain a player optimal regret of $\mathcal{O}(K\log T \left(\frac{1}Δ\right)^{2/α})$, where $K$ denotes the number of arms, $T$ is the learning horizon, and $Δ$ represents (suitably defined) players' minimum preference gap. Noticing the dependence on $Δ$ to be sub-optimal, we further improve this regret by judiciously selecting the active set of arms during exploration, which removes the dependence on $K$ in the dominant term and achieves an improved (optimal) regret guarantees in the setting where the number of arms $K$ is significantly larger than the number of players $N$. In addition, we consider adversarial markets where the observed rewards of the agents may be corrupted. We propose and analyze algorithms for robust markets with CPT as risk sensitive measure in both settings where the total corruption budget is known and where it is unknown, and establish logarithmic player-optimal regret guarantees in both cases.

</details>


### [134] [SL-S4Wave: Self-Supervised Learning of Physiological Waveforms with Structured State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19888)
*Feng Wu,Harsh Deep,Eric Lehman,Sanyam Kapoor,Guoshuai Zhao,Rahul Krishnan,Gari Clifford,Li-wei H Lehman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SL-S4Wave，一种结合对比学习与结构化状态空间模型的自我监督学习框架，用于长序列多通道生理信号（如ECG、EEG）建模，在心律失常检测等任务上显著优于现有方法，具有强标签效率和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列医学时间序列数据（如ECG）存在高采样率、多通道信号复杂、噪声大、标注数据有限等挑战。现有SSL方法（基于CNN等编码器）难以捕捉长程依赖和噪声不变特征，而现有S4架构未能捕捉多通道生理波形的独特特性。

Method: 提出SL-S4Wave框架，包含：(1) 基于结构化状态空间模型（S4）的定制化编码器，融入多层全局卷积与多尺度子核，可同时捕捉细粒度局部模式和长程时序依赖；(2) 结合对比学习的自监督学习范式，用于从无标签数据中学习鲁棒表示。

Result: 在真实ECG心律失常检测任务上：(1) 一致优于现有监督和自监督基线；(2) 显著更少的标注数据即可达到高性能，标签效率强；(3) 对长波形片段保持鲁棒性能；(4) 有效泛化到未见心律失常类型。在多个EEG任务上也取得优于强基线的性能。

Conclusion: SL-S4Wave有效解决了长序列多通道生理信号建模的难题，在标签效率、长程依赖建模和跨域泛化方面均展现出优势，且从ECG到EEG的跨模态验证了方法的通用性。

Abstract: Modeling long-sequence medical time series data, such as electrocardiograms (ECG), poses significant challenges due to high sampling rates, multichannel signal complexity, inherent noise, and limited labeled data. While recent self-supervised learning (SSL) methods, based on various encoder architectures such as convolutional neural networks, have been proposed to learn representations from unlabeled data, they often fall short in capturing long-range dependencies and noise-invariant features. Structured state space models (S4) excel at long-sequence modeling, but existing S4 architectures fail to capture the unique characteristics of multichannel physiological waveforms. In this work, we propose SL-S4Wave, a self-supervised learning framework that combines contrastive learning with a tailored encoder built on structured state space models. The encoder incorporates multi-layer global convolution using multiscale subkernels, enabling the capture of both fine-grained local patterns and long-range temporal dependencies in noisy, high-resolution multichannel waveforms. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that SL-S4Wave (1) consistently outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and self-supervised baselines in a challenging arrhythmia detection task, (2) achieves high performance with significantly fewer labeled examples, showcasing strong label efficiency, and (3) maintains robust performance on long waveform segments, highlighting its capacity to model complex temporal dynamics in long sequences that most existing approaches fail to efficiently model, and (4) transfers effectively to unseen arrhythmia types, underscoring its robust cross-domain generalization. We additionally evaluate SL-S4Wave on multiple EEG tasks, achieving superior performance over strong baselines, demonstrating generalizability of our approach beyond cardiac waveforms.

</details>


### [135] [Adversarial Bandit Optimization with Globally Bounded Perturbations to Convex Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19891)
*Zhuoyu Cheng,Kohei Hatano,Eiji Takimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗性赌博机优化问题，其中损失函数可能非凸非光滑。每轮学习者选择一个动作并仅观察到该动作的损失，损失由底层凸且β-光滑的组件和对抗性扰动组成，扰动受全局预算约束。作者将后动作扰动模型从线性损失扩展到一般凸且β-光滑损失，建立了显式刻画扰动预算影响的预期遗憾界，并通过修改标准赌博机优化算法及分析来控制扰动带来的额外遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将全局有预算的后动作扰动模型从线性损失拓展到更一般的凸且β-光滑损失函数，研究在此类对抗性扰动下学习者的遗憾保证。

Method: 修改标准赌博机优化算法，并开发分析框架来控制扰动引起的额外遗憾。

Result: 建立了显式刻画扰动预算影响的预期遗憾界；在无扰动时，结果退化为标准赌博机凸优化场景中β-光滑损失的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 成功将后动作扰动模型从线性损失推广到凸且β-光滑损失，并给出了受扰动预算影响的遗憾保证。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandit optimization in which the loss functions may be non-convex and non-smooth. In each round, the learner selects an action and observes only the loss incurred at that action. The loss consists of an underlying convex and $β$-smooth component and an adversarial perturbation that may be chosen after observing the learner's action. The perturbations are subject to a global budget controlling their cumulative magnitude over time.
  This framework extends the globally budgeted, post-action perturbation model from underlying linear losses to general convex and $β$-smooth losses. For this broader class, we establish expected regret guarantees that explicitly characterize the effect of the perturbation budget.
  To establish these guarantees, we modify a standard bandit optimization algorithm and develop an analysis that controls the additional regret caused by the perturbations. In the absence of perturbations, our results reduce to regret guarantees for the standard bandit convex optimization setting with $β$-smooth losses.

</details>


### [136] [Score Approximation for Diffusion Models on Arbitrary Low-Dimensional Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19894)
*Xinhe Mu,Zaijiu Shang,Zhaoqi Zhou,Chuan Zhou,Qi Meng,Guiying Yan,Zhiming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了得分函数可以在任意紧支撑分布（上闵可夫斯基维数d）上被ReLU网络逼近，复杂度仅随d指数增长，打破了环境维度的指数诅咒，为扩散模型处理非光滑、不规则数据提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数扩散模型的理论复杂度界限严重依赖Lipschitz连续密度或光滑流形支撑等强假设，而真实感知数据中的奇点、尖锐边界和不连续簇经常违反这些假设，亟需更通用的理论框架。

Method: 提出离散-混合（discrete-mixture）公式，利用ReLU网络在紧支撑集（上闵可夫斯基维数d）上逼近得分函数，证明复杂度仅随内在维度d指数增长。

Result: 得分函数逼近的复杂度指数依赖于数据的内在维度d而非环境维度，结合现有后向扩散SDE求解理论，表明扩散模型能自适应处理不规则、非光滑的数据结构。

Conclusion: 该通用得分逼近定理为扩散模型在真实世界生成任务中的优异表现提供了理论基础，解释了其应对复杂数据分布的能力。

Abstract: The remarkable success of score-based diffusion models has spurred significant efforts to establish their theoretical foundations. However, existing complexity bounds for score approximation rely heavily on restrictive assumptions like Lipschitz continuous densities or smooth manifold supports, which are routinely violated by the singularities, sharp boundaries, and disjoint clusters inherent to real-world perceptual data. This work establishes a universal score approximation theorem that works for any distribution supported on any compact set of upper Minkowski dimension $d$. Using a novel discrete-mixture formulation, we prove that the score function can be approximated with a ReLU network whose complexity grows exponentially only with $d$, thus breaking the exponential curse of ambient dimensionality. Combined with existing theories on accurately solving the backward diffusion SDE for arbitrary compact distributions, our work shows that diffusion models readily adapt to irregular, non-smooth data structures, explaining their competence in real-world generative tasks.

</details>


### [137] [ADaPT: Token-Level Decoupling for Efficient Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19919)
*Tingyun Li,Zishang Jiang,Jinyi Han,Xinyi Wang,Sihang Jiang,Han Xia,Zhaoqian Dai,Shuguang Ma,Fei Yu,Jiaqing Liang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADaPT通过令牌级双过程框架解耦效率与正确性信号，在训练中仅对模式选择令牌施加效率相关奖励，避免惩罚正确长推理，实现推理时效率-性能帕累托前沿的连续控制，显著降低计算成本同时保持强推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型依赖长思维链获得强性能，但统一应用长推理导致高计算成本；现有效率优化方法（缩短或混合推理策略）因序列级耦合效率与正确性信号，隐性惩罚了正确但长的推理轨迹，导致推理能力下降。

Method: 提出ADaPT框架：1）引入模式选择令牌控制快速（快思）与慢速（慢思）推理；2）仅在模式选择令牌上施加效率相关奖励，从而解耦效率与正确性信号；3）推理时通过调整模式选择令牌生成概率，实现单一模型沿效率-性能帕累托前沿的平滑连续控制。

Result: 多个基准测试实验表明，ADaPT在保持强推理性能的同时显著降低了推理成本，且能通过单一模型灵活控制效率与性能的权衡。

Conclusion: 令牌级解耦效率与正确性信号是解决长推理效率问题的有效途径，ADaPT实现了效率-性能权衡的精细控制，避免了传统方法中正确长推理被隐性惩罚的问题。

Abstract: Large reasoning models rely on long chain-of-thought to achieve strong performance, but applying such reasoning uniformly incurs high computational cost. Existing efficiency-oriented methods attempt to shorten or mix reasoning strategies, yet often degrade reasoning capability. We identify the root cause as sequence-level coupling between efficiency incentives and correctness optimization, which implicitly penalizes long but correct reasoning trajectories. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Dual-Process Thinking (ADaPT), a token-level dual-process framework that explicitly decouples efficiency and correctness signals during training. ADaPT introduces a mode-selection token to control fast and slow reasoning, applying efficiency-related rewards exclusively to this token to avoid penalizing correct long reasoning while encouraging efficiency when appropriate. Moreover, ADaPT enables precise and continuous control over the efficiency-performance trade-off at inference time: by adjusting the generation probability of the mode-selection token, a single trained model can smoothly move along the efficiency-performance Pareto frontier. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADaPT significantly reduces inference cost while maintaining strong reasoning performance across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [138] [Compositionality Emerges in a Narrow Depth-Connectivity Regime: Architecture Constraints and Solution Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19941)
*Dat H. Do,Rushi Shah,Duc V. Le,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现组合性（compositionality）仅在一个狭窄的深度-连接性最佳区间内涌现：连接性上需要特定的稀疏结构而非单纯稀疏度，深度上存在一个与目标任务相关的峰值区间。偏离这一区间，梯度下降会收敛到碎片化解而非组合解。作者提出基于相似性的剪枝和深度预测器来发现和利用这种涌现，并用理论框架解释了原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合性被认为是泛化的基础，但梯度优化训练的模型很少展现出组合性内部结构，且其形成机制尚不明确。本文旨在揭示组合性在何种条件下涌现及其背后的原理。

Method: 通过实验发现在深度和连接性两个维度上组合性涌现的狭窄区间；提出相似性剪枝（SP）方法恢复组合性连接；提出启发式深度预测器估计最佳深度；构建基于组合稀疏性、体积比论证和特征干扰边界的理论框架。

Result: 组合性仅在特定的稀疏网络连接结构（而非单纯稀疏度）下出现；深度上存在目标任务依赖的峰值区间，过浅或过深均失效；偏离该区间时梯度下降收敛到碎片化解；SP方法可恢复组合性连接，深度预测器可估计最佳深度。

Conclusion: 组合性的涌现严格依赖于深度和连接性的特定条件，存在一个狭窄的可达区间，理论框架解释了为何组合解仅在该区间内可达。

Abstract: Compositionality is believed to be the foundation for generalization, enabling models to reuse meaningful primitives in novel combinations. Yet, models trained with standard gradient-based optimization rarely, and often only weakly, exhibit compositional internal structure, and it remains unclear how or why such compositionality forms. In this work, we show that compositionality emerges in a narrow connectivity-depth sweet spot. Along the connectivity axis, compositionality only appears in some specifically sparse networks, heavily depends on which connections remain rather than on weights' sparsity alone. Along the depth axis, compositionality emerges within a narrow, target-dependent regime, peaking at specific depths, while both shallower and deeper networks fail. When either the depth or connectivity condition is violated, gradient descent silently converges to fractured solutions rather than compositional ones. To discover and exploit this emergence, we introduce (i) similarity-based pruning (SP) to recover compositional connectivity and (ii) a heuristic depth predictor to estimate where compositionality is most likely to appear. Finally, we support these empirical findings with a theoretical framework based on compositional sparsity, volume-ratio arguments, and feature-interference bounds, explaining why compositional solutions are reachable only in a narrow depth-connectivity regime.

</details>


### [139] [The Token Is a Group Element: On Lie-Algebra Attention over Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20547)
*Przemyslaw Musialski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出李代数注意力（Lie-Algebra Attention），将注意力Token定义为矩阵李群元素（裸变换，无特征负载），利用相对位姿的李代数范数作为闭式注意力分数，无需不可约表示、球谐函数、Clebsch-Gordan积或学习核，能够覆盖包括仿射全帧群在内的非紧致非阿贝尔群，在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的序列补全实验中，闭式分数匹配甚至超越学习型MLP核，参数减少50-80倍，且向量Token基线方法在不变性上相差5-12个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制中Token多为带特征向量的元素，需借助群表示（不可约表示、满射指数映射等）实现群等变，但无法处理非紧致非阿贝尔的仿射全帧群（含尺度和剪切）。作者希望构造一种无需表示论机器、能直接处理任何矩阵李群元素的注意力机制，使群等变性成为同义反复，相对几何成为内在结构。

Method: 将Token设为矩阵李群元素g_i，相对位姿为g_i^{-1} g_j，其李代数对数log(g_i^{-1} g_j)作为内在不变性；注意力分数定义为s_{ij} = -||log(g_i^{-1} g_j)||_λ^2/τ，即块加权Frobenius内积下的规范邻近核；群等变性通过对角G作用自然满足，余循环条件自动成立；无需不可约表示、球谐函数、Clebsch-Gordan积或学习核，适用于任何可选取对数图的矩阵李群。

Result: 在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)三个序列补全任务上，闭式分数匹配甚至超越学习型MLP核（SE(2)上表现更优），参数减少50-80倍；向量Token基线方法在不变性上误差达5-12个数量级。

Conclusion: 李代数注意力以最简形式实现了群等变注意力机制，无需表示论工具即可覆盖包括仿射群在内的任意矩阵李群，在参数效率和群不变性上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We place the attention token on the group: a token is an element $g_i$ of a matrix Lie group $G$ -- a bare transformation, with no feature payload and no external action $ρ(g)$ carrying it. To our knowledge this is the first attention construction whose tokens are bare matrix Lie group elements: their score is the closed-form algebra norm of the relative pose rather than a learned kernel, and it reaches the affine full-frame groups that every irrep- or surjective-exp-based method must exclude. We call it Lie-Algebra Attention. Once tokens are group elements, the rest follows with none of the usual representation-theoretic machinery. The relative geometry of a pair is canonical, $g_i^{-1} g_j$, so the pairwise invariant $w_{ij} = \log(g_i^{-1} g_j)$ is intrinsic rather than designed; equivariance under the diagonal $G$-action is tautological, and the cocycle condition holds automatically. The attention score is the negative squared algebra norm, $s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$: the canonical proximity kernel under a block-weighted Frobenius inner product, with no irreducible representations, spherical harmonics, Clebsch-Gordan products, or learned kernel. The construction applies to any matrix Lie group on a chosen logarithm chart containing the relative poses, including the non-compact non-abelian affine groups with scale and shear that no vector-token attention method reaches: neither the irrep tradition nor surjective-exp methods. Three sequence-completion experiments, on SE(2), SO(3), and Aff(2), bear this out: the closed-form score matches a learned MLP kernel on the same invariant and outperforms it on SE(2), using 50 to 80x fewer score parameters, while a vector-token baseline breaks invariance by five to twelve orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [140] [Towards Graph-Based Deep Learning for Map Generalization: Insights from Building Footprints Simplification and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19956)
*Yanning Wang,Zhiyong Zhou,Zhouyu Liu,Mengni Yu,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次探索将图深度学习应用于地图综合中的建筑物轮廓简化（节点移动预测）和聚合（链接预测），实验表明GraphSAGE在链接预测上表现较好，但节点移动预测仍有挑战，且聚合任务比简化更复杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图综合是制图学的基础任务，尤其是复杂建筑物轮廓的简化和聚合。现有方法多依赖传统规则，本文旨在探索图深度学习在这两项任务上的可行性。

Method: 将简化重构为节点移动预测，将聚合重构为链接预测，统一在一个图学习框架中；评估了GCN、GAT、GraphSAGE三种图神经网络架构在多尺度建筑物数据集上的表现。

Result: GraphSAGE在链接预测（聚合任务）上具有相对优势；节点移动预测（简化任务）仍存在精度不足的问题；聚合任务比简化任务更复杂、更具挑战性。

Conclusion: 图深度学习方法在地图综合自动化方面具有潜力但仍有局限（如数据不平衡、后处理需求），本研究为后续自动化地图综合提供了方法论方向和有价值的见解。

Abstract: Map generalization remains one of the fundamental tasks in cartography, especially for the simplification and aggregation of complex building footprints. This study presents the first exploratory application of graph-based deep learning to both tasks, reformulating simplification as node movement prediction and aggregation as link prediction within a unified graph learning framework. We evaluate representative graph neural network architectures (GCN, GAT, and GraphSAGE) on multi-scale building datasets, showing that GraphSAGE demonstrates relative strengths in link prediction accuracy, while also revealing persistent challenges in precise node movement prediction. Beyond quantitative performance, the results highlight that aggregation poses greater complexity and challenges than simplification, underscoring the difficulty of capturing higher-level spatial relationships in map generalization with current deep learning approaches. Although limitations such as data imbalance and the need for post-processing remain, the study provides valuable insights and methodological directions for advancing automated map generalization with deep learning approaches.

</details>


### [141] [Low-Energy Reduced RISC-V Instruction Subset Processor for Tsetlin Machine Inference at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19964)
*Chanda Gupta,Sanidhya Bhatia,Shaurya Priyadarshi,Himani Panwar,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Tsetlin Machine推理的领域专用RISC-V微处理器架构，通过指令精简和数据路径优化，在保持可编程性的同时，实现了相比二值神经网络最高98%的执行时间缩减和平均29.7倍的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Tsetlin Machine协处理器和加速器设计依赖紧耦合接口、微码风格编程和外部宿主机处理器，限制了灵活性和易编程性，亟需一种兼具可编程性和高效能的TM推理架构。

Method: 利用RISC-V模块化结构，通过指令分析精简指令子集，简化面向TM推理的数据路径和控制路径，设计出精简的RISC-V处理器核心。

Result: TM在CIFAR-2上精度达88.18%（BNN为60.0%）；执行时间最多减少98%；平均能耗降低29.7倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的领域专用RISC-V架构在可编程性与高效能之间取得了良好平衡，为边缘AI部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a logic-based machine learning approach that relies on simple bitwise operations and finite-state automata, which makes it attractive for edge AI deployments. Recent work has focused on co-processor and accelerator designs based on Tsetlin Machines (TMs). Although these designs achieve high performance, they typically depend on tightly coupled interfaces, microcode-style programming, and external host processors, limiting flexibility and ease of programming. In this work, we present a domain-specific RISC-V microprocessor architecture and design flow tailored for TM inference. Leveraging the modular structure of RISC-V, we design a reduced instruction subset processor that retains programmability while targeting improved performance and lower energy consumption for TM workloads. Instruction profiling is employed to guide instruction reduction, followed by datapath and control path simplifications tailored to TM inference. Both the baseline RV32IM core and the proposed reduced core are evaluated across multiple datasets and compared with Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), which serve as a hardware-efficient baseline due to their reliance on bitwise operations during inference. Results show that TM achieves comparable or higher accuracy (e.g., up to 88.18% on CIFAR-2 compared to 60.0% for BNN) while reducing execution time by up to 98% across multiple datasets. Furthermore, the proposed design achieves an average $29.7\times$ reduction in energy consumption, demonstrating its effectiveness for programmable and efficient edge AI systems.

</details>


### [142] [Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19984)
*Juntian Huang,Jurgen Kurths,Ying Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出科尔莫戈罗夫-阿诺德储层计算（KARC），用显式基函数展开替代传统储层，将储层计算与科尔莫戈罗夫-阿诺德网络（KAN）桥接，在保持KAN表达能力的同时实现高效闭式训练，在偏微分方程等挑战性基准上优于现有方法，并可集成到扩散模型进行文本到图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储层计算在捕捉长程依赖方面能力有限，超参数敏感；下一代储层计算虽去除了递归但特征维度增长过快。需要一种既能保持表达能力又能实现高效训练的新方法。

Method: 受科尔莫戈罗夫-阿诺德表示定理启发，用显式基函数展开替代传统储层，设计KARC作为KAN的轻量级实现，保留KAN的表达能力同时支持储层计算的闭式高效训练。

Result: 在包括偏微分方程在内的挑战性基准测试中，KARC在相当计算成本下优于现有储层计算方法；此外可与生成式扩散模型集成用于文本到图像生成。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了储层计算与KAN之间的原则性桥梁，实现了高效高保真的动力系统预测。

Abstract: Reservoir computing offers a lightweight framework for forecasting dynamical systems but may struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to limited representational capacity. Conventional reservoir computing recurrently uses trainable reservoirs with hyperparameter sensitivity, while the next-generation reservoir computing removes recurrence at the cost of rapidly growing feature dimensions. Here, we develop Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing (KARC), which replaces reservoirs with explicit basis-function expansions inspired by the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem. We rigorously show that KARC is a lightweight design of Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs), preserving the potential expressive capacity of KANs while admitting efficient closed-form training of reservoir computing. At comparable cost, KARC outperforms existing reservoir computing methods on challenging benchmarks including partial differential equations. It can also be integrated with generative diffusion models for text-to-image generation. This work thus establishes a principled bridge between reservoir computing and KANs, enabling efficient and high-fidelity dynamical system forecasting.

</details>


### [143] [Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR): Function-Preserving SVD Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19993)
*Nico Harder,Daniel Becking,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIR框架，通过结合激活感知和反向信号影响度量，在SVD-LLM(W)基础上添加单次ALS迭代，实现更优的低秩压缩，在≤60%参数保留时困惑度提升>18%，且减少~90%校准数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVD-based LLM压缩方法（如SVD-LLM(W)）仅考虑激活信息，忽略了反向传播信号对权重重要性的影响，限制了压缩质量。

Method: 在激活感知最优的SVD-LLM(W)基础上，提出影响感知度量（backward-signal influence），并通过单次闭式交替最小二乘（ALS）扫描，以元素方式整合影响信息，保证单调下降。

Result: 在≤60%参数保留时，困惑度比SVD-LLM(W)提升>18%；使用约90%更少的校准数据即可达到同等质量；AIR+LoRA进一步超越ACIP；参数节省转化为FLOP、峰值内存和每token延迟的收益。

Conclusion: AIR是一种层局部、可正交组合的压缩框架，通过引入影响感知显著提升了低秩压缩效率，并与端到端方法（如LoRA）兼容互补。

Abstract: We present Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR), an SVD-based LLM compression framework that guides each weight matrix's low-rank approximation with a backward-signal influence metric. Starting from the activation-aware optimum of SVD-LLM(W), AIR runs a single closed-form alternating least squares (ALS) sweep that integrates influence element-wise under a monotone-descent guarantee. AIR is layer-local and composes orthogonally with end-to-end methods: alone it exceeds ACIP, and AIR+LoRA outperforms it further. AIR improves perplexity over SVD-LLM(W) by >18% at <=60% parameter retention, matches its quality with ~90% less calibration data, and turns parameter savings into FLOP, peak-memory, and per-token latency gains.

</details>


### [144] [Connect the Dots: Training LLMs for Long-Lifecycle Agents with Cross-Domain Generalization Via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20002)
*Yanxi Chen,Weijie Shi,Yuexiang Xie,Boyi Hu,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“连接点”(CoD)框架，通过端到端强化学习训练大语言模型掌握元能力，使其在长期部署中能持续探索环境、从自身经验学习并迭代更新上下文，从而在后续任务中表现更好。实验验证了该框架的有效性和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体在面对长期部署场景（需连续解决大量任务并持续学习和自我更新）时缺乏一种关键的元能力——即“连接点”能力。作者旨在通过一个通用框架训练LLM获得这种能力，使其能够在环境中持续探索、从自身经验中学习并迭代更新上下文。

Method: 提出了CoD框架，包含两个主要组件：(1) 端到端强化学习的算法设计和基础设施，使用长rollout序列交错执行求解任务和更新上下文的情节；(2) 专门设计的任务和环境，用于在训练中激励和引出目标元能力，并在评估中忠实衡量进展。具体实现采用了GRPO风格的RL算法并配合细粒度信用分配。

Result: 实证结果验证了端到端RL训练在CoD设置中的有效性，并展示了所引出元能力的跨域泛化潜力——包括训练域内泛化、跨不同领域泛化，以及从CoD到Ralph-loop设置的泛化。

Conclusion: 本工作连接了多条先前的研究线，为推进LLM和AI智能体的发展开辟了新机会，并发布了开源实现以促进后续研究和应用。

Abstract: This work presents a general framework for training large language models (LLMs) to "Connect the Dots" (CoD), a meta-capability required by long-lifecycle agents: as an LLM-based AI agent gets deployed in an environment, it solves a long sequence of tasks while continuously exploring the environment, learning from its own experiences, and iteratively self-updating its context about the environment, thereby achieving progressively better performance on future tasks conditioned on the updated context. Major components of the CoD framework include: (1) algorithm design and infrastructure for end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) with long rollout sequences interleaving solve-task and update-context episodes; (2) tasks and environments for incentivizing and eliciting the targeted meta-capability in LLMs during training, as well as for faithfully measuring progress during evaluation. We present proof-of-concept implementations of the CoD framework, including a GRPO-style RL algorithm with fine-grained credit assignment, as well as tasks and environments tailored to the targeted meta-capability (rather than domain-specific LLM capabilities or standard task-by-task RL). Empirical results validate the efficacy of end-to-end RL training in the CoD setting, and demonstrate the potential for out-of-distribution generalization -- within the training domains, across different domains, and from CoD to Ralph-loop settings -- of the elicited meta-capability. Our investigation of CoD connects several lines of prior works, and opens up new opportunities for advancing LLMs and AI agents. To facilitate further research and applications, we release our implementations at \url{https://github.com/agentscope-ai/Trinity-RFT/tree/research/cod/examples/research_cod}.

</details>


### [145] [StreamKL: Fast and Memory-Efficient KL Divergence for Boosting Attention Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20005)
*Guangda Liu,Yiquan Wang,Chengwei Li,Wenhao Chen,Jing Lin,Yiwu Yao,Danning Ke,Wenchao Ding,Jieru Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamKL提出了一种融合的GPU核函数，用于高效计算注意力分布的KL散度，将内存占用从O(N_Q N_K)降至O(1)，并实现了前向和后向分别高达43倍和14倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力蒸馏方法需要物化两个注意力分布（O(N_Q N_K)内存和IO成本），在处理长上下文时变得不可行。本文旨在解决这一二次复杂度的内存瓶颈问题。

Method: StreamKL推导了耦合双分布KL散度的在线计算公式，实现了单次前向融合核函数，通过片上SRAM流式处理查询-键块；后向传播时逐块重计算注意力概率，避免存储二次中间结果。

Result: 前向传播相比基线方法加速高达43倍，后向传播加速高达14倍；将额外HBM占用从O(N_Q N_K)降至O(1)，支持在单GPU上进行长上下文蒸馏。

Conclusion: StreamKL是首个用于注意力KL散度的融合GPU原语，通过在线计算和流式处理彻底消除了注意力蒸馏中的二次内存和IO开销，使长上下文注意力蒸馏成为可能。

Abstract: Attention distillation, which trains one attention distribution to match another by minimizing their Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, is widely used in knowledge distillation, model compression, continual learning, and sparse-attention LLM training. However, existing approaches materialize both attention distributions before computing the KL reduction, incurring $O(N_QN_K)$ memory and IO costs that become prohibitive at long context lengths. We present StreamKL, the first fused GPU primitive for attention KL divergence that eliminates this quadratic materialization. StreamKL derives a novel online formulation for the coupled two-distribution KL reduction, enabling a single one-pass forward kernel that streams query-key tiles through on-chip SRAM. For the backward pass, StreamKL recomputes attention probabilities tile-by-tile, avoiding storage of quadratic intermediates. We further design and implement efficient GPU kernels with dedicated optimizations. Experiments show StreamKL delivers up to $43\times$ and $14\times$ speedups over baseline methods in the forward and backward passes, respectively. Most importantly, StreamKL reduces the extra HBM footprint of attention distillation from $O(N_QN_K)$ to $O(1)$, enabling long-context distillation on a single GPU.

</details>


### [146] [VIMPO: Value-Implicit Policy Optimization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20008)
*Zhewei Kang,Aosong Feng,Sergey Levine,Dawn Song,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIMPO是一种无需评论家（critic-free）的策略优化方法，通过KL正则化强化学习的最优性条件推导出策略隐含的价值函数，在数学推理任务上优于GRPO，尤其在噪声奖励场景下表现更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在提升大语言模型推理能力时面临权衡：基于组相对的方法（如GRPO）简单但信用分配粗糙（轨迹级优势），而Actor-Critic方法提供稠密信号但需要学习不稳定的价值函数。本文旨在设计一种既无需评论家又能实现细粒度信用分配的方法。

Method: 从KL正则化强化学习的最优性条件出发，推导出策略隐含的价值函数（policy-implied value function）。对于自回归生成，该价值递归可用策略-参考对数比表示，并以轨迹末端无未来奖励为终止条件锚定。由此得到无需评论家的价值损失（通过结果级可验证奖励）和优势函数，将奖励融入（通过价值损失）与策略改进（通过PPO风格的Actor更新）分离。

Result: 在MATH-500、AIME 2024、AIME 2025、OlympiadBench等数学RLVR基准上，VIMPO优于GRPO，尤其在竞赛级评估上提升更显著。在噪声奖励场景下，VIMPO保持了一致优势。

Conclusion: VIMPO通过策略隐含价值优化实现了细粒度信用分配，同时保留了无评论家训练的实践简洁性，为提升LLM推理能力提供了一种新的有效范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a central tool for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, but current methods face a trade-off between simplicity and credit assignment. Group-relative methods such as GRPO avoid training a critic, but typically assign a trajectory-level advantage to every token. Actor-critic methods provide denser learning signals, but require a learned value function with its own training instability. We introduce VIMPO, a critic-free policy optimization method that derives a policy-implied value function from the optimality conditions of KL-regularized reinforcement learning. For autoregressive generation, the resulting value recurrence can be written in terms of policy-reference log-ratios and anchored by the terminal condition that no future reward remains at the end of a trajectory. This gives a simple value loss that incorporates outcome-level verifiable rewards without training a critic. The same derivation also yields a critic-free actor advantage, allowing VIMPO to separate reward incorporation through the value loss from policy improvement through a PPO-style actor update. On mathematical RLVR benchmarks, VIMPO improves over GRPO across MATH-500, AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and OlympiadBench, with especially larger gains on competition-style evaluations. Under noisy rewards, VIMPO retains a consistent advantage over GRPO, suggesting that policy-implied value optimization can provide finer credit assignment while preserving the practical simplicity of critic-free training.

</details>


### [147] [Self-Adaptive Scale Handling for Forecasting Time Series with Scale Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20010)
*Xu Zhang,Zhengang Huang,Yunzhi Wu,Xun Lu,Erpeng Qi,Yunkai Chen,Zhongya Xue,Peng Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应尺度处理（AS）模块，通过学习自适应尺度因子解决时间序列预测中不同序列数量级差异（尺度异质性）的问题，在真实基金销售数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测研究主要针对尺度同质数据，但工业场景中不同序列常存在数量级差异（尺度异质性）。现有归一化方法（全局归一化压缩低尺度信号，窗口归一化破坏语义可辨别性并放大逆缩放误差）无法有效处理这一问题，因此需要一种能自适应处理尺度异质性的方法。

Method: 提出自适应尺度处理（AS）模块，包含两个子模块：1) 尺度校准（SC）：通过神经网络校准先验均值缩放因子；2) 缩放选择（SS）：决定是否应用校准或保留原始因子，避免过度校准。

Result: 在蚂蚁财富和支付宝的真实基金销售数据集上，AS模块可无缝集成到主流TSF模型中，并持续提升其预测性能。

Conclusion: AS模块能有效处理时间序列中的尺度异质性问题，在保持语义可辨别性的同时减少逆缩放误差，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Current time series forecasting (TSF) research predominantly focuses on scale-homogeneous data, where different time series share similar numerical magnitude ranges. However, in real-world industrial scenarios such as financial product sales, different time series often differ by orders of magnitude (scale heterogeneity). Since these series share similar temporal patterns, joint modeling is desirable for better data utilization, yet existing scaling methods either compress low-scale signals (global normalization) or destroy semantic discriminability and amplify inverse-scaling errors (window-based scaling). This paper proposes a self-Adaptive Scale-handling (AS) module that learns adaptive scale factors tailored to each input, preserving semantic discriminability while reducing inverse-scaling errors. AS consists of Scale Calibrating (SC), which calibrates prior mean scaling factors through neural networks, and Scaling Selection (SS), which decides whether to apply calibration or retain the original factor, avoiding over-calibration.
