In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [185] [Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20537)
*Liang Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出执行状态胶囊（Execution-State Capsules）和FlashRT运行时，用于低延迟、小批量的设备端物理AI服务场景。与主流KV缓存复用不同，胶囊机制在承诺边界上对整个可恢复执行状态（包括KV、循环状态、卷积状态等）进行图绑定的检查点与恢复，支持快照、恢复、分支和回滚。在RTX 5090上，GPU驻留的快照与恢复达到亚毫秒级，TTFT加速比从2k token的3.9x提升至16k token的27x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统（如分页KV缓存、基数树缓存）仅管理KV缓存这一单一执行状态片段，在高吞吐、高并发场景下有效，但不适用于低延迟、小批量、频繁分支/重置/中断的设备端物理AI服务（如交互式LLM代理、语音系统、机器人策略）。

Method: 提出执行状态胶囊（Execution-State Capsules）——图绑定的检查点与恢复机制，在承诺边界保存完整可恢复状态；设计FlashRT白盒运行时，基于NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续静态缓冲区上执行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址；通过关闭的命名缓冲区集合实现整个执行边界的快照、恢复、分支与回滚。

Result: 在RTX 5090上，胶囊恢复在存储状态级别字节精确、贪心解码下token一致；KV-only消融实验出现发散，证明循环状态是关键负载；GPU驻留快照与恢复为亚毫秒级；TTFT加速比从2k token的3.9x增长到16k token的27x。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上验证了相同的正确性与结构性质。

Conclusion: 执行状态胶囊不是高吞吐KV缓存服务的替代方案，而是定义了一个互补的、以延迟优先的服务端点，用于显式执行状态复用。

Abstract: Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.

</details>


### [186] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于分摊层次贝叶斯预测推断，通过将先验信息表示为上下文数据集的prefix，使Transformer能够跨先验族自适应预测，在多种难度评估中匹配贝叶斯oracle性能且速度快数个数量级，并在实际时空温度预测任务中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯预测推断在不确定性量化、数据效率和鲁棒泛化方面具有优势，但精确推断难以处理，可扩展近似方法要么计算昂贵，要么需要限制性建模假设导致预测性能下降。现有分摊方法（如Prior-Data Fitted模型）与训练先验的支撑集紧密耦合，缺乏在测试时自适应新先验的机制，导致在分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架用于分摊层次贝叶斯预测推断：将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀（prefix），训练一个Transformer在由先验任务和目标任务组成的序列上学习，从而学会跨先验族自适应预测。

Result: 在包含元分布外先验和高维潜在结构先验的系列评估中，该方法匹配了贝叶斯oracle预测器的性能，同时速度提升数个数量级；在真实世界时空温度预测基准上也展示了实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的多任务上下文学习框架有效解决了现有分摊贝叶斯推断方法对先验分布偏移鲁棒性不足的问题，实现了高效且泛化的贝叶斯预测推断。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [187] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于可预测性的隐私"框架，通过衡量攻击者在观察算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息预测能力的增量来量化隐私泄露，与差分隐私（DP）在一般情况下不可比较，但在最坏情况下可蕴含互信息DP，并利用广义矩估计（GMM）分析渐近可预测性，提出可预测性校准的输出扰动方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）虽然提供了严格的个体级隐私保障，但其最坏情况性质导致了昂贵的隐私-精度权衡，需要更细粒度的隐私度量框架来反映实际攻击者知识并改善隐私-效用权衡。

Method: 提出"可预测性"（predictability）框架，量化攻击者在观察算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息预测能力的增量收益；利用广义矩估计（GMM）分析在平稳、遍历、混合过程中生成受损数据时的渐近可预测性；基于此分析提出可预测性校准的输出扰动方案用于经验风险最小化（ERM）。

Result: 可预测性与差分隐私在一般情况下不可比较（各自可小对方大）；在最坏情况（除一人外全部受损、所有二元查询敏感）下，可预测性蕴含互信息DP；可预测性提供了针对特定敏感信息和特定攻击者模型的更细粒度隐私度量。

Conclusion: 可预测性框架与差分隐私互补，可与之协同使用以提供细粒度的隐私控制，尤其适用于已知部分数据被泄露（受损）的实际场景。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [188] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多重校准中随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要的问题，给出了一个达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的确定性预测器算法，并推广到结果不可区分性、全知预测器和泛预测器等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重校准是可信机器学习的基本要求，此前达到极小极大最优样本复杂度$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的预测器都是随机化的，确定性预测器的样本复杂度显著更差。随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要是CLNR26等文献中明确提出的开放问题。

Method: 提出了一个能够输出确定性预测器的多重校准算法，达到极小极大最优样本复杂度；然后将该算法推广到有限或有限覆盖测试集合下的结果不可区分性（OI）场景，进而得到最优确定性的全知预测器和泛预测器。

Result: 给出了一个确定性预测器的多重校准算法，达到极小极大最优样本复杂度$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$，解决了随机化是否必要的开放问题；同时推广到OI、全知预测器和泛预测器场景，解决了OKK25和BHHLZ25中提出的开放问题。

Conclusion: 随机化对于多重校准的最优样本复杂度并非必要，确定性预测器也可以达到相同的极小极大最优速率。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [189] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究DiffusionGemma（一种扩散语言模型）的推理透明度，发现尽管其表面不透明序列深度是自回归Gemma 4的28.6倍，但通过在可解释的token瓶颈中映射信息流，可将不透明序列深度降至Gemma 4的1.1倍。然而，扩散模型的算法透明度更难实现，因为所有token预测在每一步都可能改变。作者通过可解释性案例研究发现了非时序推理、token/序列模糊和中间上下文推理等扩散特有现象。最后，DiffusionGemma的可监控性与Gemma 4相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理透明度对于理解模型决策、减轻误用和异常行为调试至关重要。DiffusionGemma在连续潜在空间中执行大部分计算，可能降低透明度，因此需要系统研究其透明度特性。

Method: 将透明度分解为变量透明度（中间计算快照的可理解性）和算法透明度（利用快照重建推理过程的能力）。通过信息流映射将去噪步骤间的信息通过可解释token瓶颈传递，降低不透明序列深度。通过可解释性案例研究分析扩散特有现象。

Result: 变量透明度：映射后不透明序列深度从28.6X降至1.1X；算法透明度：发现非时序推理、token/序列模糊、中间上下文推理等新现象；可监控性：与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型的变量透明度可通过信息瓶颈方法大幅提升，但算法透明度面临更大挑战，需进一步研究扩散特有的推理机制。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Design Considerations for Phase Modulation in Testable Photonic Systems and Co-packaged Optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19674)
*Pratishtha Agnihotri,Priyank Kalla,Steve Blair*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文比较了硅光集成芯片中热光调制与载流子电光调制两种相位调制机制，评估了它们在马赫-曾德尔和微环调制器中的消光比、调谐效率、功耗和调制带宽等性能，揭示了调制速度、能耗与可调谐性之间的权衡关系，为可测试性设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硅光集成芯片复杂度不断提升，测试与校准越来越依赖高效的相位调制机制，需要系统比较不同调制方法的优劣以支持设计决策。

Method: 设计了基于热光效应和载流子电光效应的马赫-曾德尔调制器与微环调制器，对比评估了消光比、调谐效率、功耗和调制带宽等关键指标。

Result: 识别了调制速度、能耗与调谐可控性之间的关键权衡关系，明确了两种方法在不同工作区间内的相对优势与局限性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为面向集成测试与校准需求的可扩展硅光系统中相位调制策略的选择提供了实用的设计指导。

Abstract: As silicon photonic integrated circuits (PICs) scale in complexity, testing and calibration increasingly depend on effective phase modulation mechanisms. This work compares thermally induced phase modulation and carrier-based electrical modulation in Mach-Zehnder and microring modulators. The devices are designed and evaluated for extinction ratio, tuning efficiency, power consumption, and modulation bandwidth. The study identifies key trade-offs among modulation speed, energy consumption, and tuning controllability that directly influence the suitability of these methods for test signal generation and calibration tasks. The results highlight the relative advantages and limitations of thermal and electrical approaches across different operating regimes. These findings provide practical design guidance for selecting phase modulation strategies in scalable silicon photonic systems with integrated test and calibration requirements.

</details>


### [191] [A Novel FeFET Differential Bit-Cell With Hybrid Volatile and Non-Volatile Memory Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19918)
*Jianze Wang,Wei Zhang,Xuanyao Fong*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由两个交叉耦合FeFET和两个存取晶体管组成的4T nvSRAM位单元，面积小于传统6T SRAM，支持易失性和非易失性模式切换，非易失模式下存储功耗0.13μW、存储时间2ns，无需显式备份恢复操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CMOS SRAM漏电功耗高，新兴非易失存储器写入延迟大，现有nvSRAM设计方案通常需要备份恢复操作且单元面积开销大，亟需一种面积更小、无需备份恢复的高效nvSRAM方案。

Method: 采用一对交叉耦合FeFET替代传统CMOS交叉耦合反相器，搭配两个存取晶体管构成4T结构；通过调整写入条件配置为易失性或非易失性工作模式，无需显式备份恢复操作。

Result: 4T位单元面积小于传统6T SRAM及先前多种nvSRAM设计；非易失模式下存储功耗0.13μW，存储时间2ns，无需备份恢复操作。

Conclusion: 所提4T FeFET nvSRAM位单元在面积、功耗和延迟方面具有优势，且可作为交叉耦合增益单元扩展应用，为未来低功耗非易失存储设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Non-volatile SRAM (nvSRAM) designs have been investigated to address the high leakage power of CMOS-based SRAM and the large write latency of emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) technologies. However, prior nvSRAM designs that combine SRAM with eNVM devices typically require backup and restore (B\&R) operations and incur significant cell-area overhead. Here, we propose a differential memory bit-cell consisting of a pair of cross-coupled ferroelectric field-effect transistors (FeFETs) and a pair of access transistors, resulting in a four-transistor (4T) structure, which is smaller than conventional 6T SRAM and many prior nvSRAM designs. The proposed bit-cell can be configured to operate in either volatile or non-volatile mode by adjusting the write conditions. In the non-volatile mode, the proposed nvSRAM achieves a store power of 0.13~$μ$W with a 2~ns store time, and no explicit B\&R operation is required. The proposed bit-cell can also be viewed as a cross-coupled gain cell, enabling further applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Weight Adaptation for Improving Parallel Performance of Adaptive Stochastic Natural Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19861)
*Yutaro Yamada,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出WA-ASNG算法，通过在ASNG中引入权重自适应机制，解决了概率模型进化算法在大种群规模下权重参数难以设定的问题，实验表明其在二值优化问题中优于PBIL和ASNG，且对强噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率模型进化算法中权重参数是常见超参数，但随着并行评估时间密集型任务的需求增加，如何为大种群规模设定合适的权重仍不明确。

Method: WA-ASNG通过累积自然梯度估计更新方向的信号，然后利用梯度上升最大化该信号，从而自适应更新权重参数；学习率自适应保证期望目标值单调改进的充分条件，权重自适应则旨在最大化这种改进。

Result: 在种群规模25至100的二值优化问题中，WA-ASNG优于PBIL和ASNG，且在强噪声环境下仍能高效运行。

Conclusion: 权重自适应机制能够有效提升概率模型进化算法在大种群规模下的优化性能，WA-ASNG在多种设置下均表现优越。

Abstract: Probabilistic model-based evolutionary algorithms are promising for black-box optimization. Specifically, the adaptive stochastic natural gradient (ASNG) adaptively updates its learning rate, a typical hyperparameter in probabilistic model-based evolutionary algorithms, thereby realizing efficient and robust optimization. Although weight parameters are common hyperparameters, with the increasing demand for parallel evaluation of time-consuming tasks, it remains unclear how to set suitable weights for larger population sizes. In this paper, we propose Weight Adaptation ASNG (WA-ASNG), which incorporates a weight adaptation mechanism into ASNG. We calculated the estimated signal of the update direction from the accumulations of the natural gradient. Then, to maximize the signal, WA-ASNG adaptively updates its weight parameters by a gradient ascent over the optimization. While the learning rate adaptation plays a role in satisfying a sufficient condition for monotonic improvement of the expected objective value, the mechanism of weight adaptation is intended to maximize this improvement. The experimental results demonstrate that WA-ASNG outperforms PBIL and ASNG across various settings with population sizes ranging from 25 to 100 for binary optimization problems. Furthermore, WA-ASNG can perform efficiently in the presence of strong noise. Our code is available at https://github.com/shiralab/WA-ASNG .

</details>


### [193] [Hybrid ANN-SNN Pipeline with Local Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20151)
*Denis Larionov,Khairutin Shtanchaev,Mikhail Kiselev,Mikhail Korovin,Ivan Tugoy*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合ANN-SNN流水线，利用预训练人工神经网络（EfficientNet编码器）与脉冲神经网络分类器（CoLaNET）结合，通过速率编码将激活转换为脉冲序列，并使用局部生物启发学习规则训练SNN分类器，在64类ImageNet上达到99.09%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用预训练ANN的强大嵌入能力来提升SNN的性能，同时避免端到端梯度传播，实现生物合理性与高性能的结合。

Method: 构建混合架构：预训练EfficientNet作为编码器提取特征，通过速率编码将激活值转换为脉冲序列，送入CoLaNET脉冲分类器，使用局部生物启发学习规则（而非反向传播）训练SNN部分。

Result: 在64类ImageNet基准上达到99.09%的准确率，性能与传统深度网络相当。

Conclusion: 提出了一种生物合理且高效的框架，能够将强大的预训练编码器适配到下游SNN任务，证明了混合ANN-SNN方法的可行性。

Abstract: This work proposes a hybrid ANN-SNN pipeline that effectively leverages the rich embeddings of pretrained artificial neural networks (ANNs) to enable high-performance spiking neural networks (SNNs). The architecture couples a pretrained EfficientNet encoder with a CoLaNET spiking classifier. We convert the encoder's activations into spike trains via rate-coding and train the subsequent SNN classifier using local, biologically inspired learning rules, bypassing end-to-end gradient propagation. This approach achieves 99.09% accuracy on a 64-class ImageNet benchmark, demonstrating performance on par with conventional deep networks. The work presents a biologically plausible and efficient framework for adapting powerful pretrained encoders to downstream spiking neural network tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Effect Systems as Abstract Interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19686)
*Colin S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文建立了效应系统与抽象解释之间的形式化关系，展示了效应量代数可以嵌入抽象域，并从事件发生角度将效应量代数恢复为一种抽象解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态推理形式（如类型系统和效应系统）与抽象解释之间的形式化关系研究不足，特别是效应系统作为抽象解释的一般情况尚未解决。

Method: 首先将效应量代数嵌入到抽象域中，然后从事件发生的角度将效应量代数的一般形式恢复为一种抽象解释。

Result: 建立了效应系统与抽象解释之间的形式化对应关系，表明效应系统本质上可以被视为一种在事件发生上的抽象解释。

Conclusion: 效应系统与抽象解释之间存在深层的形式化联系，这为理解效应系统的语义基础提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Many forms of static reasoning about program behaviours are known in the literature, yet formal relationships are studied surprisingly infrequently. While most type systems are well-known to be captured by abstract interpretations, the situation for type-and-effect systems is, in the general case, unsettled despite strong hypotheses and occasional framing of effect systems as abstract interpretations.
  We develop a formal relationship between abstract interpretations and a general class of effect systems. First, we describe an embedding of effect quantales into abstract domains. Second, we recover the general form of an effect quantale as an abstract interpretation -- not on states or values, but on event occurrences.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [REMOP: REmote-Memory-aware OPerator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19576)
*Shiquan Zhang,Yunhao Mao,Yuqiu Zhang,Gengrui Zhang,Jeyhun Karimov,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出REMOP框架，通过传输轮次感知的算子内内存管理策略，优化远程内存场景下的查询处理性能，在DuckDB中实现最高97%的传输轮次减少和48%的算子运行时间降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程和分离式内存层级扩展了分析型数据库引擎的有效内存容量，但重塑了内存溢出查询处理的成本结构：远程内存传输不仅包含数据传输时间，还包含每次传输的固定往返延迟。传统的磁盘溢出优化策略（最小化总I/O量）在远程内存场景下可能因触发过多传输轮次而效果不佳。

Method: REMOP将传输轮次纳入延迟成本模型，推导出算子特定的缓冲区划分策略，并在DuckDB中为阻塞嵌套循环连接、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接三种算子实现了该方法。

Result: 在双节点计算-内存测试平台上的评估显示：在溢出密集型微基准测试中，传输轮次减少最高达97%，算子运行时间降低最高达48%；端到端测试中，溢出TPC-H和TPC-DS查询的平均运行时间分别降低22.7%和26.4%。

Conclusion: REMOP证明了在远程内存场景下，以传输轮次感知的方式重新设计算子内内存策略，能够显著优于传统的总I/O量最小化策略，为分离式内存架构下的数据库查询优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Remote and disaggregated memory tiers expand the effective memory capacity of analytical database engines, but they also reshape the cost structure of out-of-memory query processing. When an operator spills beyond local DRAM, moving pages to remote memory incurs both data-transfer time and a fixed round-trip latency per transfer. Classical operator analyses and buffer-allocation heuristics primarily target disk spilling by minimizing total I/O volume. Under remote memory, these strategies can be suboptimal because they may trigger excessive transfer rounds. We present REMOP, a remote-memory-aware operator optimization framework that uses transfer-round-aware intra-operator memory policies to improve out-of-memory execution under tight memory budgets. REMOP introduces the number of transfer rounds into the latency cost model and derives operator-specific buffer-partitioning strategies, instantiating the approach for blocked nested-loop join, external merge sort, and external hash join in DuckDB. Our evaluation on a two-node compute-memory testbed shows that REMOP reduces transfer rounds by up to 97% and operator runtime by up to 48% on spill-heavy microbenchmarks, and lowers the average runtime of spilling TPC-H and TPC-DS queries by 22.7% and 26.4% end-to-end.

</details>


### [196] [DeQL: A Decision Query Language for Prescriptive Analytics over Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19751)
*Matteo Brucato,Fjodor Kholodkov,Soren Little,Jakob Mayer,Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DeQL是一种扩展SQL的决策查询语言，通过CREATE CANDIDATES和DECIDE两个核心构造，使用户能从关系数据中定义选项空间并声明决策变量、约束和目标，实现"用户声明优化什么，引擎决定如何求解"的声明式决策建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SQL无法直接表达决策优化问题（如资源分配、调度等），用户需要借助外部求解器或编写过程式代码，导致决策逻辑分散、难以维护。DeQL旨在将决策优化能力融入SQL，使决策查询像传统数据查询一样声明式化。

Method: 在SQL中引入两个核心构造：CREATE CANDIDATES从关系数据源定义候选方案空间；DECIDE声明决策变量、命名约束和目标函数。执行模型由引擎自动选择合适的求解策略。支持子集选择、分配、调度等多类决策问题，并扩展至不确定性优化、内联模型评分及时间/质量有界求解。

Result: 给出了完整的语言规范，包括设计原则、语法、形式化文法及执行模型，并通过多个示例（子集选择、分配、调度、多级聚合决策）展示了表达能力，同时支持优化不确定性、内联模型评分和时质有界求解等扩展。

Conclusion: 这是DeQL的首版规范，核心构造已固定，语言仍在积极开发中，后续版本将在此基础上迭代扩展。

Abstract: DeQL (Decision Query Language) extends SQL to express decision queries: given options drawn from relational data, constraints from policy, and a measurable objective, a DeQL query computes the best course of action. Two constructs carry the extension: CREATE CANDIDATES, which defines the space of options from relational sources, and DECIDE, which declares decision variables, named constraints, and an objective over them. The design follows SQL's principles: the user states what to optimize while the engine chooses how to solve it, every query consumes and produces relations, and the structure of a problem stays visible to the engine. This document specifies the language (its design principles, syntax, formal grammar, and execution model) with examples spanning subset selection, allocation, assignment, scheduling, and decisions at multiple levels of aggregation, and extensions for optimization under uncertainty, inline model scoring, and time- and quality-bounded solving. It is the first version of the specification; the language is under active development, and this version fixes the core constructs on which later revisions will build.

</details>


### [197] [Policy-aware Vector Search: A Vision for Fine Grained Access Control in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19803)
*Lakshmi Sahithi Yalamarthi,Primal Pappachan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出向量数据库中的细粒度访问控制（FGAC）问题，形式化定义了FGAC策略模型与实施问题，比较了不同实施策略，并指出了政策感知向量搜索的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库在安全敏感场景（如RAG和AI管道）中使用日益广泛，但其安全能力有限，特别是缺乏细粒度访问控制（FGAC）支持，导致无法确保数据访问符合用户特定策略。

Method: 形式化定义了向量数据库中FGAC策略模型及实施问题，比较了多种实施策略，并通过初步实验验证了不同策略的效果。

Result: 揭示了FGAC策略正确执行、高ANN搜索召回率与低查询延迟之间存在内在张力，给出了初步发现。

Conclusion: 指出政策感知向量搜索是一个有待深入研究的方向，明确了关键开放挑战。

Abstract: Vector databases are increasingly used in security sensitive contexts with Retrieval Augmented Generation and organizational AI pipelines; however, their security capabilities remain limited. Specifically, Fine-grained Access Control (FGAC) which is required to ensure that data access adheres to user-specific policies is not fully supported in modern vector databases. Unlike relational databases, vector databases combine structured and unstructured attributes to provide semantic, approximate query results, which complicates FGAC implementation. This creates an inherent tension between enforcing FGAC policies correctly, achieving high ANN search recall and maintaining low query latency. In this paper, we present a vision for Policy-aware Vector Search by formalizing the FGAC policy model in vector databases as well as the enforcement problem. We compare various enforcement strategies, present preliminary findings, and identify key open challenges for future research in policy-aware vector search.

</details>


### [198] [Query-aware Routing for Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19898)
*Qianqian Xiong,Mengxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种查询感知的路由框架，通过轻量级ML模型预测各过滤式ANN方法的召回率，并借助离线基准表选择最优方法，实现了在多个数据集上召回率与QPS的最佳平衡，且延迟开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过滤式ANN方法在跨数据集和谓词类型时无单一方法占优，且同一数据集内不同查询的最佳方法也不同，亟需一种能根据查询特点动态选择最优方法的路由机制。

Method: 提出查询感知路由框架：1) 轻量级ML模型预测各候选方法在查询上的召回率；2) 路由器查询离线基准表（映射方法与参数设置到实测召回率和QPS），选择召回率-QPS权衡最优的方法；3) 通过消融实验将22个候选特征缩减至3个；4) 采用回归而非分类作为预测目标以提升准确性。

Result: 在6个真实数据集上训练，在5个未见过的验证数据集上测试，路由器在所有验证集上相比现有过滤式ANN基线实现了最先进的召回率与QPS平衡，且延迟开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 查询感知路由框架有效解决了过滤式ANN方法选择问题，以极小开销实现跨数据集和查询的自适应最优方法选择，达到SOTA性能。

Abstract: Filtered ANN search, which combines vector similarity with attribute predicates, is a core primitive in modern vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation. We benchmark all major categorical filtered ANN methods across multiple datasets under three predicates and find that no single method dominates. Moreover, even within a single dataset and predicate type, the best method for a query can vary. Therefore, we propose a query-aware routing framework. A lightweight ML model predicts each candidate method's recall on the query, and the router consults an offline benchmark table that maps every method and parameter setting to its measured recall and QPS, then selects the method with the best recall--QPS trade-off. Our ablation study narrows 22 candidate features to a minimal set of three and we adopt regression rather than classification as the prediction target to sharpen accuracy. Our model is trained on six real-world datasets and applied to five unseen validation datasets. The final result shows that our router achieves state-of-the-art recall and QPS balance across all five validation datasets compared to existing filtered ANN baselines, while incurring negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [199] [The Bi-Channel Networking Paradigm for Database Systems in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19969)
*Georg Kreuzmayr,Muhammad El-Hindi,Benjamin Wagner,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对现代高速云网络中传统内核TCP栈成为数据库性能瓶颈的问题，本文提出双通道通信范式，将用户态UDP（高性能数据路径）与内核TCP（可靠控制路径）结合，实现分布式数据库系统的高效网络通信，在200 Gbit/s分布式shuffle和百万级消息/秒的复制键值存储中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云网络速度大幅提升，内核TCP栈的CPU开销成为数据库性能瓶颈；而直接用用户态UDP替代TCP又需要重新实现可靠性、有序性等关键保证。现有方案无法在低开销和高可靠性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出双通道通信范式：将通信分离为两条通道——高性能数据路径（使用用户态UDP，利用现代NIC能力）处理延迟敏感和带宽敏感的操作，以及可靠控制路径（使用内核TCP）处理协调和恢复。两种通道协同工作，兼顾性能与可靠性。

Result: 在分布式shuffle场景中，仅用3个CPU核心即可饱和200 Gbit/s网络带宽；在复制键值存储场景中，可处理每秒数百万条消息，验证了该范式的高效性和简洁性。

Conclusion: 数据库系统不应再将网络视为黑盒，而应与数据库操作协同设计。双通道范式通过分离数据路径和控制路径，在现代高速网络下实现了高性能与可靠性的统一，且实现简洁。

Abstract: When network links were slow, cloud and distributed database systems could rely on generic kernel abstractions and treat network communication as a black box. With today's fast cloud networks, this approach breaks down: database performance becomes limited by the CPU overhead of the kernel TCP stack. Replacing TCP with user-space UDP can reduce this overhead, but it requires reimplementing essential guarantees, such as reliability and ordering. To solve this conundrum, database systems should no longer treat networking as a black box but co-design it with database operations. We propose the bi-channel paradigm for database systems, which separates communication into two channels: A high-performance data path for latency- and bandwidth-sensitive operations, and a reliable control path for coordination and recovery. We implement the paradigm by combining user-space UDP and kernel-based TCP, though other stack combinations are possible. This design exploits modern NIC capabilities while preserving TCP's reliability. We demonstrate the paradigm's efficiency and simplicity in two representative settings: a distributed shuffle saturating 200 Gbit/s with three CPU cores, and a replicated key-value store processing millions of messages per second.

</details>


### [200] [AgenticDB: Agentic Performance Reconfiguration for Database Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20318)
*Xinyue Yang,Chaozheng Wang,Chen Zheng,Heng Zhang,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出AgenticDB，一个基于智能体的数据库工作负载重配置框架，通过与运行环境的交互式反馈实现DBMS和OS层面的自优化重配置，在MySQL和PostgreSQL上相比最强基线平均提升118.1%性能，并将达到最优配置的时间缩短22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库自动调优方法将调优视为对DBMS knob值的迭代搜索，导致执行成本高、过早缩小配置空间，难以满足实际需求：从系统反馈诊断运行时瓶颈、探索OS级重配置机会、安全执行变更以及从历史试验和任务中学习。

Method: AgenticDB实现了一个上下文驱动的交互框架，包含：1) 与目标数据库环境交互，提出DBMS和OS级别的变更；2) 在安全约束下执行变更；3) 观察工作负载性能和运行时状态；4) 利用执行反馈指导后续决策。以此实现运行时瓶颈诊断、更广的DBMS+OS重配置空间探索、不安全操作规避以及跨任务经验积累。

Result: 在MySQL和PostgreSQL上使用YCSB、Sysbench和TPC-H工作负载进行实验，AgenticDB在所有评估工作负载上取得最佳最终性能，相比最强基线平均提升118.1%，并将达到最优配置的累计时间缩短22.6%。结果还表明其OS级动作空间、鲁棒的执行生命周期和增强记忆的规划机制贡献了更有效和实用的数据库重配置。

Conclusion: AgenticDB将数据库调优转化为一个通过运行时反馈迭代改进后续决策的自优化重配置过程，证明了融合DBMS和OS级动作空间、鲁棒执行机制与记忆增强规划的有效性，为实际数据库部署提供了更实用、更高效的自动调优方案。

Abstract: Database configuration tuning is critical for workload performance, but practical tuning on real deployments remains difficult. Existing automatic tuners mostly formulate tuning as iterative search over DBMS knob values. This formulation leads to high execution cost, prematurely narrows the configuration space, and leaves practical requirements insufficiently addressed: diagnosing runtime bottlenecks from system feedback, exploring OS-level reconfiguration opportunities, executing changes robustly, and learning from previous trials and tasks.
  We propose AgenticDB, an agentic framework for database workload reconfiguration. AgenticDB implements a context-grounded harness that interacts with the target database environment by proposing DBMS- and OS-level changes, applying them under safety constraints, observing workload performance and runtime states, and using execution feedback to guide subsequent decisions. This runtime interaction enables AgenticDB to diagnose bottlenecks, explore a broader DBMS- and OS-level reconfiguration space, avoid unsafe or unsupported actions, and accumulate experience within and across reconfiguration tasks. As a result, AgenticDB turns database tuning into a self-refining reconfiguration process in which runtime feedback iteratively improves later decisions.
  We conduct extensive experiments on MySQL and PostgreSQL using YCSB, Sysbench, and TPC-H workloads. The results show that AgenticDB achieves the best final performance on all evaluated workloads, improving over the strongest baseline by 118.1% on average and reducing aggregate time-to-best by 22.6%. The results also demonstrate that its OS-level action space, robust execution lifecycle, and memory-enhanced planning contribute to more effective and practical database reconfiguration.

</details>


### [201] [Caching for Dollars, Not Hits: An Exact Offline Reference for Cloud-Egress Caching and the Crossover That Decides When It Pays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20539)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了云对象存储场景下以美元成本为优化目标的缓存策略基准。针对异构缺失成本，给出了离线最优策略的多项式时间精确解（统一大小页缓存）及近似边界（可变大小，误差约4%）。通过实验发现了异质性遗憾定律、竞争边界及闭合形式交叉点s* = GET费用/出站费率，并基于Twitter真实轨迹验证了成本感知缓存的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缓存优化目标是降低缺失率，但在云对象存储中，每次GET请求和出站字节数都产生费用，不同对象的缺失成本差异可达数千倍。经典缓存策略优化目标错误，缺乏以美元成本为目标的缓存策略的实际性能基准。

Method: 针对统一大小页缓存，通过积分区间线性规划给出离线的多项式时间精确最优解；针对可变大小（NP难），将基于命中率的流式离线边界扩展至成本目标（cost-FOO），误差约4%。基于这些基准，对比LRU和GreedyDual等启发式策略在真实云价格下的美元遗憾值。

Result: (i) 异质性遗憾定律：LRU的美元遗憾值随缺失成本离散度上升（Spearman 0.87），而成本感知的GreedyDual可降至约十分之一；(ii) 竞争边界：当预算恰好覆盖高成本工作集时GreedyDual的残余遗憾接近零，否则为开放区间；(iii) 闭合形式交叉点s* = GET_fee/egress_rate（S3约4 KB，GCS约330 B），可预测哪些部署需要成本感知缓存。Twitter轨迹验证了价格向量可跨越s*切换缓存策略的优化目标。

Conclusion: 云对象存储缓存应以美元成本而非缺失率为优化目标，GreedyDual等成本感知策略在多数场景下显著优于LRU。s*提供了简单的决策准则判断是否需要美元感知缓存。本文提供了可复现的账单保真基准。

Abstract: When a cache miss fetches from cloud object storage, the bill is per GET request and per byte of egress, not latency. Classic caching minimizes the miss rate, the wrong objective: a rarely but expensively fetched object can cost thousands of times more dollars than a frequently but cheaply fetched one. Generalized-caching theory bounds the miss-cost objective, but no reported benchmark measures how far deployed heuristics sit from the dollar-optimal offline policy on real cloud prices. We supply that reference. For uniform-size page caches with heterogeneous miss costs the offline dollar-optimum is exact in polynomial time via an integral interval linear program -- validated against brute force; variable sizes are NP-hard, so we extend the flow-based offline bound from the hit-ratio objective to dollars (cost-FOO), tight to about four percent. Against this reference we find: (i) a heterogeneity-regret law -- LRU's dollar-regret rises with miss-cost dispersion (Spearman 0.87) while cost-aware GreedyDual cuts it to roughly a tenth; (ii) a contention frontier -- GreedyDual's residual regret collapses to near zero exactly when the budget fits the expensive working set, and is the open slice otherwise; and (iii) a closed-form crossover s* = GET_fee/egress_rate (about 4 KB on S3, 330 B on GCS) that predicts which deployments need dollar-aware caching at all. On a real Twitter trace the price vector alone moves the workload across s*, shifting the regime as predicted. The artifact is a reproducible billing-faithful benchmark; heuristics and bounds it builds on are prior work, credited.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Secure Coding Drift in LLM-Assisted Post-Quantum Cryptography Development: A Gamified Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19474)
*R. D. N. Shakya,C. P. Wijesiriwardana,S. M. Vidanagamachchi,Nalin A. G. Arachchilage*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出后量子密码学（PQC）向AI辅助开发过渡中出现的“安全编码漂移”概念，并设计了一种游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架来应对这一风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着PQC的推广，其实现需要严格的常数时间执行、侧信道抵抗等安全要求；而LLM被广泛用于软件开发，但常生成不安全的密码学代码。现有工作关注静态漏洞，忽视了人机交互导致的长期安全退化现象，因此需要新的风险模型和缓解方案。

Method: 提出Secure Coding Drift in PQC模型，将安全风险概念化为纵向行为现象；设计游戏化LLM增强框架，集成对抗性评估、行为反馈和安全评分，将LLM从被动助手转变为主动安全协作者。

Result: 提出了一个新型的社会技术漏洞模型，并设计了一套将安全机制嵌入开发流程的框架。

Conclusion: 该框架有望在AI辅助开发环境中推动更安全的PQC实现，将LLM重构为主动的安全协作者而非被动的代码生成器。

Abstract: The transition to Post Quantum Cryptography (PQC) introduces considerable implementation complexity, requiring strict adherence to constant-time execution, side channel resistance, and precise parametrisation. Simultaneously, large language models (LLMs) are heavily embedded in software development workflows, including cryptographic engineering. While LLMs improve productivity, evidence shows that they frequently generate insecure or suboptimal code, particularly in security critical domains. This paper introduces Secure Coding Drift in PQC, a novel socio technical vulnerability model capturing the gradual degradation of secure coding practices due to sustained reliance on LLM-generated code. Unlike prior work that focuses on static vulnerabilities, we conceptualise security risk as a longitudinal behavioural phenomenon rising from human AI interaction. To mitigate this, we propose a gamified, LLM augmented secure coding framework that embeds adversarial evaluation, behavioural feedback, and security scoring into development workflows. Our approach reframes LLMs from passive assistants into active security co-pilots, contributing toward safer PQC implementation in AI mediated environments.

</details>


### [203] [FloatDoor: Platform-Triggered Backdoors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19535)
*Nils Loose,Jonas Sander,Felix Mächtle,Thomas Eisenbarth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FloatDoor，首个输入无关、平台触发的后门攻击，利用LLM在不同部署平台上的数值差异，通过两个轻量LoRA适配器在目标平台上触发恶意行为，同时保持模型在其他平台正常。实验在Qwen3-4B上验证，涵盖NVIDIA GPU、Google TPU等多种硬件平台，可可靠诱导代码漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在敏感场景（如软件工程）中部署，但同一模型因浮点运算非结合性和不同内核实现，在不同平台上产生可测量的输出差异。作者研究这种平台依赖变异性带来的安全影响，发现了一种新的攻击面。

Method: FloatDoor包含两个轻量LoRA适配器：一个放大跨平台数值发散，另一个将产生的平台特征绑定到恶意下游任务。攻击利用模型审计与部署之间的TOCTOU（检查时间与使用时间）差距。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上针对NVIDIA GPU、Google TPU、AWS Graviton、Alibaba Yitian-710等多个平台验证，FloatDoor能可靠地在目标平台上诱导可被利用的代码漏洞，同时保持模型整体效用。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一类新的LLM部署攻击，强调了在敏感的LLM驱动应用中建立可信模型供应链的紧迫性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in sensitive settings such as software engineering, where their outputs directly shape downstream artifacts. Recent work has shown that an identical model can produce measurably different outputs depending on the deployment platform, a consequence of non-associative floating-point arithmetic and divergent kernel implementations. We study the security implications of this platform-dependent variability and uncover a novel attack surface on LLM deployments. We introduce FloatDoor, the first input-independent, platform-triggered backdoor attack against generative LLMs. The compromised model exhibits adversary-chosen behavior when served on a target platform and is otherwise benign. FloatDoor is realized through two lightweight LoRA adapters, one that amplifies inter-platform numerical divergence and one that binds the resulting platform signature to a malicious downstream task, while leaving aggregate model utility largely intact. FloatDoor exploits a pronounced time-of-check, time-of-use gap between model auditing and serving. We demonstrate FloatDoor on Qwen3-4B across a broad range of deployment targets, including NVIDIA GPUs, Google TPUs, AWS Graviton, and Alibaba Yitian-710. As a final case study, we show that FloatDoor reliably induces exploitable code vulnerabilities on a chosen target platform. Our results establish a new class of attacks on LLM deployments and underscore the pressing need for trusted model supply chains in sensitive, LLM-powered applications.

</details>


### [204] [G-Lox: Group-Adaptive, Privacy-Preserving Bridge Distribution with Two-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19620)
*Baigang Chen,Nicholas Hopper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: G-Lox是一个群自适应桥接分发系统，在保持Lox风格的分发器盲性的同时，通过双服务器隐私墙实现隐藏的、有状态的群级别自适应，使用DPF/FSS协议和安全两方计算支持阻塞报告、传输感知重分配和隐私保护的群分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Lox系统虽能实现分发器盲性，但缺乏群级别的自适应能力；而引入自适应逻辑会泄露群标识符和桥接分配信息。G-Lox旨在在不牺牲隐私的前提下实现有状态的群自适应分配。

Method: 采用双服务器隐私墙架构，将自适应分配逻辑置于其后，确保单一服务器无法获知群标识符或群到桥接的分配；利用双服务器DPF/FSS协议和安全两方计算实现私有状态访问和状态依赖更新。

Result: 在C++/EMP实现中，私有状态访问在状态大小达2^16时仍保持低KiB级别的通信开销；当M=1024时，客户端发送1,968字节、接收1,280字节，单次迭代约0.25秒。仿真表明G-Lox在群特定阻塞和Sybil枚举场景下优于Lox和rBridge类基线。

Conclusion: G-Lox能够在维持分发器盲性的前提下，通过隐私保护的群自适应机制显著提升系统在面对群特定阻塞和Sybil攻击时的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present G-Lox (group-adaptive Lox), a bridge-distribution system that preserves Lox-style distributor blindness while enabling hidden, stateful group-level adaptation. G-Lox places adaptive assignment logic behind a two-server privacy wall, so no single server learns group identifiers or group-to-bridge assignments. Private state access and state-dependent updates use two-server DPF/FSS protocols and secure two-party computation, supporting blockage reporting, transport-aware reassignment, and privacy-preserving group splitting.
  We evaluate G-Lox through system measurements and policy simulation. In our C++/EMP implementation over real TCP sockets, private state access has low client-visible overhead: across state sizes up to 2^16, communication remains in the low-KiB range per iteration. At M=1024, the client sends 1,968 bytes, receives 1,280 bytes, and completes an iteration in about 0.25 s. Simulations with group-specific blocking and Sybil enumeration show that G-Lox improves robustness over Lox- and rBridge-like baselines among systems that maintain broad issuance.

</details>


### [205] [PUFFERDOS: Efficient and Effective Attack String Generation for Regular Expression Denial of Service Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19654)
*Shangzhi Xu,Ziqi Ding,Xiao Cheng,Yuekang Li,Nan Sun,Benjamin Turnbull,Shuangxiang Kan,Siqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PUFFERDOS，一种能够在现实长度预算内生成有效ReDoS攻击字符串并经过程序级验证的工具，解决了现有攻击字符串生成技术在实际应用中有效性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReDoS攻击字符串生成技术存在两个主要缺陷：生成的攻击字符串通常假设不切实际的输入长度预算，且未在程序级别验证攻击的有效性和效率，导致许多字符串在实际程序中无法触发漏洞，限制了其实用价值。

Method: PUFFERDOS首先基于观察和形式化验证定义三种易受攻击的模式，然后根据这些模式进行综合合成生成攻击字符串，最后通过ReDoS特有的组合式符号执行（concolic execution）对字符串进行精炼和验证，确保其在真实程序中的可利用性。

Result: PUFFERDOS能够在现实长度预算内生成有效的攻击字符串，并在程序级别得到验证，实现了对实际程序中ReDoS漏洞的有效利用。

Conclusion: PUFFERDOS通过结合模式定义、综合合成与组合式符号执行，显著提升了ReDoS攻击字符串的实用性和有效性，弥补了现有技术在现实场景中的不足。

Abstract: ReDoS attacks constitute a critical class of resource-exhaustion vulnerabilities. In such attacks, adversaries exploit the pathological worst-case execution behavior of regular expression (regex) engines to induce highly asymmetric computational workloads, ultimately exhausting system resources and degrading service availability. To protect systems against ReDoS attacks, numerous detection techniques have been proposed that simulate the attack process by generating attack strings to proactively exploit ReDoS vulnerabilities at the early development stage and facilitate remediation. Existing techniques broadly fall into two classes: static analyses that search for pathological regex structures, and dynamic exploration methods that synthesize candidate attack strings. However, the generated attack strings are often impractical for real-world exploitation because they usually assume unrealistic input-length budgets and do not validate the effectiveness and efficiency of the attack at the program level. Therefore, many generated strings fail to trigger vulnerable regexes when applied to real-world programs, further limiting the practical utility. To address these shortcomings, we introduce an effective and efficient attack string generator, PUFFERDOS, designed to synthesize attack inputs that are both feasible within realistic length budgets and validated at the program level, enabling effective exploitation of ReDoS vulnerabilities in real-world programs. Specifically, we first define three vulnerable patterns based on our observation and formal verification. According to the patterns, PUFFERDOS conducts a synthesis technique to generate attack strings, and then refines and validates the strings with ReDoS-specific compositional concolic execution to guarantee real-world exploitability.

</details>


### [206] [A Layered Security Framework Against Prompt Injection in RAG-Based Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19660)
*Gulshan Saleem,Nisar Ahmed,Muhammad Imran Zaman,Ali Hassan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三层防御框架，用于拦截LLM推理流水线中的直接和间接提示注入攻击。该框架包含输入筛选、基于来源的指令层次和输出审计三个层级，通过连续审计循环实现自适应更新。在5,080个样本上的评估显示，攻击成功率从71.4%降至11.3%，误报率4.8%，延迟开销61.2ms，性能优于单层基线及现有防护系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入被OWASP Top 10列为LLM应用最关键的漏洞，但现有防御仅在孤立流水线阶段运行且不完整：输入过滤器无法检查检索文档，输出监控器无法阻止恶意载荷到达模型。因此，RAG聊天机器人仍易受间接注入攻击，一个被污染的知识库文档可危害所有检索到它的用户。

Method: 提出三层防御框架：第一层使用规则模式库和微调语义异常分类器筛查用户输入；第二层在上下文组装阶段强制执行基于来源的指令层次，防止检索内容覆盖操作策略；第三层在输出交付前使用策略规则引擎和语义漂移检测器审计模型输出。连续审计循环汇总结构化日志支持分类器重训练以适应新兴攻击模式。框架为模型无关的中间件，不修改底层LLM。

Result: 在GPT-4o、Llama 3和Mistral 7B上使用5,080个样本评估，攻击成功率从71.4%降至11.3%，比最优单层基线高27.3个百分点，比已发布的护栏系统高23.8个百分点，误报率4.8%，中位延迟开销61.2ms。

Conclusion: 消融实验确认三个层级提供互补保护，且组合效果超过各自贡献之和。框架以最小延迟开销实现了对LLM提示注入攻击的有效防御。

Abstract: Prompt injection is ranked as the most critical vulnerability in large language model (LLM) deployments by the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications, yet existing defenses operate at isolated pipeline stages and remain incomplete. Input filters cannot inspect retrieved documents, while output monitors cannot prevent malicious payloads from reaching the model. Consequently, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) chatbots remain vulnerable to indirect injection, where a poisoned knowledge-base document compromises every user whose query retrieves it. We present a three-layer framework that intercepts both direct and indirect prompt injection throughout the inference pipeline. Layer 1 screens user input using a rule-based pattern library and a fine-tuned semantic anomaly classifier. Layer 2 enforces a provenance-based instruction hierarchy during context assembly, preventing retrieved content from overriding operator policy. Layer 3 audits model output using a policy rule engine and semantic drift detector before delivery. A continuous audit loop aggregates structured logs and supports retraining to adapt the classifier to emerging attack patterns. The framework is model-agnostic and deploys as middleware without modifying the underlying LLM. Evaluation on 5,080 samples across GPT-4o, Llama 3, and Mistral 7B shows that the framework reduces Attack Success Rate (ASR) from 71.4\% to 11.3\%, outperforming the best single-layer baseline by 27.3 percentage points and a published guardrail system by 23.8 percentage points, while maintaining a 4.8\% false positive rate and a median latency overhead of 61.2 ms. Ablation studies confirm that all three layers provide complementary protection and that their combined effect exceeds the sum of individual contributions.

</details>


### [207] [When Global Gating Is Enough: Admission-Time Hubness Control in Anisotropic Vector Retrieval Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19692)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak,Tarun Kumar Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种在RAG系统中通过准入时间控制（使用哨兵查询评分）来防御向量中心性投毒攻击的方法，实现了高召回率和低误报率，且计算开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中向量中心性（hubness）导致少数文档成为大量查询的最近邻，使得单个恶意注入文档能够影响多个不相关查询，存在投毒攻击风险。现有基于周期性反向kNN扫描的防御存在暴露窗口和重复全语料库计算的问题。

Method: 提出准入时间控制机制：在文档插入前，使用哨兵查询集合对候选文档进行评分，隔离类似中心性（hub-like）的文档。设计全局门控（global gate）和主题门控（per-topic gate）两种变体，采用增量维护阈值，支持近似索引。

Result: 在10万文档语料库上，全局门控在决定性嵌入空间点实现召回率1.0（有效范围≥0.92），HotFlip攻击下召回率0.91±0.07，误报率1%。准入延迟增加约3.1%，评分性能在10^6向量范围内保持平稳，近似索引下仅有1.2%的决策翻转且不涉及攻击。

Conclusion: 准入时间控制是一种有效的RAG投毒防御方法，全局门控性能优异，主题门控无可靠收益；该方法计算开销低、可增量维护，适合大规模部署；对于自然或紧密领域中心性，溯源可作为补充。

Abstract: Vector hubness, where a few points become nearest neighbors of many queries, creates a poisoning risk in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): one injected document can influence unrelated requests. Existing defenses use periodic reverse-kNN scans, leaving an exposure window and repeated corpus-wide work. We study admission-time control, scoring each candidate against sentinel queries and quarantining hub-like documents before insertion. Across two 100,000-document corpora, five encoders, and disjoint attacker and defender query sets, a global gate achieves recall 1.0 at the decisive embedding-space point (>=0.92 across the effective range) and 0.91 +/- 0.07 on HotFlip attacks, with 1% false positives on general documents. A per-topic gate provides no reliable benefit, consistent with anisotropy coupling local and global visibility. Thresholds are maintained incrementally, with corpus-size-independent insertion cost and amortized deletion cost. On HNSW, admission adds about 3.1% to ingestion latency, scoring remains flat to 10^6 vectors, and 1.2% of decisions flip under approximate indexing, none involving attacks. Provenance complements the gate for natural or tight-domain hubs.

</details>


### [208] [SafeSpec: Fast and Safe LLM via Dynamic Reflective Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19755)
*Haotian Xu,Zeyang Zhang,Linbao Li,Huadi Zheng,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeSpec提出了一种安全感知的推测推理框架，通过在目标模型上附加轻量级安全头，在验证阶段联合评估语义有效性和安全性，并采用回滚和安全引导的反射式多采样来恢复安全延续，从而解决推测解码与安全防御之间的根本性不兼容问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防御方法与推测解码存在根本性不兼容：它们要么引入额外计算开销，要么破坏草稿-验证机制，从而抵消了加速收益。

Method: 在目标模型上附加轻量级潜在安全头，在单次前向传播中联合评估语义有效性和安全性；检测到不安全生成时，采用回滚和安全引导的反射式多采样恢复安全延续；将越狱攻击建模为生成轨迹上的分布偏移。

Result: 在多个模型和对抗基准上，SafeSpec显著提升了安全-效率权衡。在Qwen3-32B上，攻击成功率降低15%，同时在良性负载上保持2.06倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: 推测加速和推理时安全可以联合优化，实现了安全性与效率的双赢。

Abstract: Speculative inference accelerates large language model (LLM) decoding but provides no inherent safety guarantees. Existing safety defenses are largely incompatible with speculative inference: they either introduce additional computation or disrupt the draft-verify mechanism, negating acceleration benefits. This reveals a fundamental incompatibility between current safety methods and speculative decoding. We propose SafeSpec, a safety-aware speculative inference framework that integrates risk estimation directly into the verification process. SafeSpec attaches a lightweight latent safety head to the target model to jointly evaluate semantic validity and safety in a single forward pass. When unsafe generations are detected, SafeSpec applies rollback and safety-guided reflective multi-sampling to recover safe continuations rather than terminating generation. We model jailbreak attacks as distributional shifts over generative trajectories, where adversarial prompts increase the probability of harmful continuations without eliminating safe ones. Under this model, SafeSpec performs risk-aware trajectory recovery within the speculative decoding process. Across multiple models and adversarial benchmarks, SafeSpec achieves a substantially improved safety-efficiency trade-off. On Qwen3-32B, SafeSpec reduces attack success rates by 15% while preserving a 2.06x inference speedup on benign workloads, demonstrating that speculative acceleration and inference-time safety can be jointly optimized.

</details>


### [209] [DISARM: Target Electronic Device Informed Mitigation of Software Runtime Side-Channel Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19807)
*Tasneem Suha,Tanzim Mahfuz,Rima Asmar Awad,Prabuddha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DISARM提出了一种联合硬件-软件的运行时侧信道漏洞缓解方法，利用真实嵌入式设备的时序值生成针对性软件修复，在22个标准基准测试中优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时侧信道攻击缓解方案大多未考虑目标程序运行的底层硬件，导致过度修复、修复不足甚至失败。

Method: 提出DISARM，一种结合硬件时序测量与软件修复的联合方法，支持C、C++和Java源代码，利用真实嵌入式设备的执行时序值生成针对性的软件修复。

Result: 在22个标准基准测试和5种不同嵌入式/边缘设备上，DISARM在执行时间开销、代码大小开销和正确性方面均优于PENDULUM和DifFuzzAR等现有方案。

Conclusion: 考虑底层硬件特性的联合硬件-软件方法能更有效地缓解运行时侧信道漏洞，避免过度修复或修复不足的问题。

Abstract: Program runtime or timing attacks exploit variations in a program's execution times to extract sensitive information from the program (e.g. encryption keys, sensitive variable data, intellectual property). State-of-the-art solutions to runtime side-channel attacks attempt to balance the execution time of the sensitive code for different control flow paths to eliminate the timing leakage. However, during the mitigation process, most techniques do not consider the underlying hardware or device on which the target program is supposed to run on. This can lead to over-fixing (unnecessary extra operations), under-fixing (not solving the imbalance properly), and even failures. We propose DISARM, a joint hardware-software methodology (unlike any existing solution) for mitigating runtime side-channel vulnerabilities that utilizes timing values from real embedded devices to generate targeted software fixes. We implement DISARM to support C, C++, and Java source codes and validate it across 22 standard benchmarks. DISARM outperforms state-of-the-art solutions such as PENDULUM and DifFuzzAR in terms of execution time overhead, code size overhead, and correctness on five different embedded or edge devices.

</details>


### [210] [Heterogeneous LLM Debate Under Adversarial Peers: Honest Gains, Replacement Costs, and Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19826)
*Prashanti Nilayam,Kiran Kumar Ramanna,Prashil Tumbade,Sankalp Nayak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究异构LLM辩论中，异构同伴对诚实智能体修正行为的影响。实验发现，诚实的异构同伴能显著降低有害修正率（如Llama-3.1-70B在MATH-hard上从89%降至35%），而敌对的异构同伴则逆转此效果。当存在同族敌对同伴时，诚实的异构同伴还能减少正确答案丢失率（从31%降至6%），说明异构性既是攻击面也是防御手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构LLM辩论的动机在于不同模型可以相互纠正错误，但同一交互过程既可能带来纠正也可能引入对抗性影响。本文旨在量化哪种效应占主导地位，通过追踪异构同伴如何改变诚实智能体的修订行为。

Method: 通过匹配面板（同族基线、诚实混合、敌对混合）和污染面板（已存在同族恶意同伴）进行对比实验，覆盖四个模型家族和三个推理基准。测量诚实智能体修改答案的频率及修改是纠正性还是有害性。

Result: 诚实的异构同伴能显著降低有害修正率（Llama-3.1-70B在MATH-hard上从89%降至35%），敌对异构同伴则将其恢复至90%。条件率会掩盖对弱防御者的伤害，但辩论结束时的翻转率能暴露问题。当已有同族敌对同伴时，诚实的异构同伴能将初始正确项的翻转率从31%降至6%。

Conclusion: 异构性不仅是攻击面，在已有敌手时也是一种防御手段。模式在模型家族和基准上保持符号一致，幅度随防御者-基准组合变化。

Abstract: Heterogeneous LLM debate is motivated by the promise that diverse peers correct one another, but the same exchange that carries correction also carries adversarial influence. We measure which dominates by tracking how a heterogeneous peer changes the honest agents' revision behavior: how often they change their answer, and whether the change is corrective or harmful. We compare matched panels (homogeneous baseline, honest-mixed, and adversarial-mixed) and contaminated panels in which a malicious same-family peer is already present, spanning four model families and three reasoning benchmarks. An honest heterogeneous peer sharply lowers harmful revision, and an adversarial one reverses it. For Llama-3.1-70B defenders on MATH-hard, the honest-slot harmful-revision rate falls from 89% in the homogeneous panel to 35% with an honest peer, and an adversarial peer returns it to 90%. The conditional rate hides this damage on weak defenders, but the end-of-debate flip rate exposes it. The pattern keeps its sign across families and benchmarks while its magnitude varies with the defender-benchmark regime. We also measure the effects when an adversarial same-family peer is already present: an honest heterogeneous peer lowers both harmful revision and the rate at which initially-correct answers are lost. On the same Llama-3.1-70B setting, the added honest peer cuts the flip rate on initially-correct items from 31% under a same-family adversary to 6%. Heterogeneity is therefore not only an attack surface but, when an adversary is already present, also a defense.

</details>


### [211] [Low-Cost Multi-Precision Systolic Arrays for Accelerating FHE NTTs on AI ASICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19866)
*George Alexakis,Dimitrios Schoinianakis,Giorgos Dimitrakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对FHE在AI硬件（TPU）上因精度不匹配导致的性能瓶颈，本文提出一种微修改的多精度脉动阵列，在矩阵乘法的统一数据流内原生执行全精度输出重建，实现NTT加速1.33倍以上，且硬件开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE在AI硬件上加速时存在精度不匹配问题：TPU优化于8位低精度计算，而FHE及其核心NTT变换需要高精度。现有方法通过矩阵分解在低精度矩阵引擎上执行NTT，但全精度结果重建需要移位加法累加，与矩阵乘法数据流不匹配，被迫卸载到向量处理器，造成严重性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种微修改的多精度脉动阵列架构，在矩阵引擎内部原生执行全精度输出重建，与低精度矩阵乘法在统一数据流下同步进行。在7nm工艺下使用OpenRoad综合实现。

Result: 在128x128矩阵引擎上，对于2^12到2^16的变换规模，NTT执行速度提升至少1.33倍；硬件开销极低（通过7nm OpenRoad综合验证）。

Conclusion: 该设计成功使标准AI硬件能够高效支持高精度FHE加速，在不破坏矩阵乘法数据流的前提下解决了精度与效率的矛盾。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) ensures robust data privacy but suffers from prohibitive computational overhead. Accelerating FHE on AI hardware like Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) is promising, yet fundamentally limited by a precision mismatch: TPUs are optimized for 8-bit arithmetic, whereas FHE and its critical parts such as the Number Theoretic Transform (NTT), demand high precision. Current approaches bridge this gap using matrix decomposition to execute NTT computations on low-precision matrix engines. However, reconstructing the full-precision results requires shift-and-add accumulation that does not match the dataflow of matrix multiplication. This forces offloading full-precision reconstruction from matrix engines to vector processors that disrupts the matrix multiplication dataflow, creating significant performance bottleneck. To resolve this limitation, we propose a minimally modified multi-precision systolic array that performs full-precision output reconstruction natively within the array in sync with low-precision matrix multiplication under a uniform dataflow. Synthesized at 7nm with OpenRoad, our design incurs negligible hardware overhead. Cycle-accurate simulations using SCALE-Sim demonstrate that natively executing NTTs on the proposed architecture achieves at least 1.33x speedup, for transform sizes 2^12 to 2^16 on 128x128 matrix engines, successfully enabling standard AI hardware to support high-precision FHE acceleration.

</details>


### [212] [FFinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19887)
*Chaeyun Kim,Daeyoung Park,Junghwan Kim,Jinyoung Jeong,Eunji Song,Yongtaek Lim,Minwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FinRED是一个由金融专家指导的红队框架，用于金融大语言模型安全评估，采用双层分类法映射全球标准至金融特定威胁，通过专家验证的流程生成上下文丰富的红队行为提示，并提供专家验证的评估指标，已在韩国金融安全研究所监管沙盒中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准针对通用对抗场景，缺乏金融特定风险评估，金融大语言模型面临监管合规违规、欺诈助力和系统性信任侵蚀等需针对性评估的问题。

Method: 提出FinRED框架：1) 双层分类法映射全球标准（如FATF、EU DORA）至金融威胁；2) 可扩展流水线将真实金融文档转换为红队行为提示（种子）；3) 专家定义模式；4) 专家验证的金融特定评估指标，超越免责声明检查，减少关键假阴性从28到12。

Result: 专家验证确认种子的合理性和真实性；评估指标更贴近人类专家，显著降低关键假阴性（28→12）；已部署于韩国金融安全研究院监管沙盒，用于真实金融服务中的生成式AI安全评估。

Conclusion: FinRED与国际风险管理与信息安全标准（如ISO/IEC 27001）对齐，为金融LLM提供针对性安全评估方案；数据集、生成流水线、提示模板和评估框架对合格研究者开放，以缓解双重使用风险。

Abstract: Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.

</details>


### [213] [AutoTam: Specifying Secure Protocol Implementations with Tamarin Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19937)
*Johannes Wilson,Mikael Asplund,Niklas Johansson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“语言优先”的形式化验证方法，通过领域特定语言实现协议，自动生成Tamarin模型并验证迹属性，同时集成符号执行分析内存安全。在签名Diffie-Hellman和WireGuard协议上验证了可行性，WireGuard实现可互操作且性能可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证对于密码协议安全至关重要，但现有工具建模门槛高，且将验证结果迁移到具体实现需要专家知识。现有方法难以被缺乏形式化背景的实践者使用。

Method: 提出语言优先的方法：设计领域特定语言（DSL）用于协议实现，自动生成Tamarin验证模型；证明验证的通用迹属性可回传至实现；集成符号执行分析内存安全。

Result: 实现了签名Diffie-Hellman和WireGuard协议的精确模型；WireGuard实现与现有实现可互操作，性能可接受；通过符号执行和Tamarin模型验证证明了安全性。

Conclusion: 语言优先的方法降低了形式化验证的门槛，使得非专家也能进行协议验证，并且验证结果可直接映射到具体实现，同时保证了内存安全。

Abstract: Formal verification is a challenging but important task for ensuring the security of cryptographic protocols. While modern protocol verification tools significantly reduce verification effort, modelling remains challenging to practitioners without a background in formal verification. In addition, transferring verification results to a concrete protocol implementation requires expert knowledge.
  In this paper, we present a novel language-first method for verification of trace properties using a domain-specific language for protocol implementations. We target the Tamarin prover for verification, and we prove that verified universal trace properties translate back to the implementation. We additionally integrate symbolic execution in order to analyse the memory safety of protocol implementations. We use our tool to implement and generate accurate models for a signed Diffie-Hellman protocol, and for the WireGuard VPN protocol. Our WireGuard implementation is interoperable with existing implementations when using our interpreter, and achieves acceptable performance. We formally prove our implementations secure using a combination of symbolic execution and verification of the generated Tamarin models.

</details>


### [214] [A Measurement Study of Cryptographic Misuse in Embodied AI Mobile Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19983)
*Junchao Li,Xuelei Wang,Yuhang Huang,Qi Wang,Boyang Ma,Xuelong Dai,Minghui Xu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对具身AI移动应用中的密码学误用进行了大规模测量研究，构建了包含507个真实应用的EAIAppZoo基准，发现12,975个密码学误用案例（精确率80.74%），揭示了由EAI特定工程约束驱动的结构性安全缺陷，并展示了这些漏洞如何使攻击者劫持物理控制通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要集中在具身AI设备和云端基础设施，而移动控制层作为关键攻击面尚未被充分探索。随着EAI移动应用从辅助用户界面发展为主动控制路径组件，移动端密码学安全直接关联到网络物理系统的可信性，因此亟需填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建EAIAppZoo基准（507个跨6个EAI域的真实应用），采用自动化语义感知分析管线，测量五大类密码学失效模式的普遍性和特征。

Result: 检测到12,975个密码学误用发现（精确率80.74%）；密码学失效由EAI特定的工程约束驱动而非随机开发者错误；延迟敏感的控制路径系统性地削弱了传输保护；对离线设备配置和遗留IoT SDK的依赖加剧了本地硬编码认证凭证问题。

Conclusion: 移动应用已成为网络物理系统中一个脆弱但被忽视的密码学信任边界，移动端的密码学缺陷可绕过名义上的网络保护，使攻击者拦截命令通道并劫持EAI实体的物理控制。

Abstract: Embodied AI (EAI) mobile applications are evolving from auxiliary user interfaces into active control-path components, directly linking mobile-side cryptographic security to cyber-physical trust. Despite this shift, existing security research predominantly focuses on embodied AI devices and cloud infrastructures, leaving the mobile control layer largely unexplored as a critical attack surface. To bridge this gap, we present the first large-scale measurement study of cryptographic misuse within the EAI mobile ecosystem. We construct EAIAppZoo, a benchmark of 507 real-world applications across six EAI domains, and employ an automated semantic-aware analysis pipeline to measure the prevalence and characteristics of five major cryptographic failure modes. Our measurement yields 12,975 misuse findings (with an evaluated precision of 80.74\%), revealing that these cryptographic failures are driven by EAI-specific engineering constraints rather than random developer errors. We uncover structural security trade-offs: latency-sensitive control paths systematically weaken transport protection, while the heavy reliance on offline device provisioning and legacy IoT SDKs exacerbates the local hardcoding of authentication credentials. Through real-world case studies, we demonstrate how these mobile-side cryptographic flaws bypass nominal network protections, enabling adversaries to intercept command channels and hijack the physical control of EAI entities. Ultimately, our findings highlight that mobile applications have become a fragile, yet overlooked, cryptographic trust boundary in cyber-physical systems.

</details>


### [215] [Accelerating Trust Convergence in IIoT: A ML Approach for Dynamic Network Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20214)
*Aymen Bouferroum,Valeria Loscri,Abderrahim Benslimane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的信任收敛加速（TCA）方法，通过预测信任收敛所需时间单位并动态调整信任模型中的转移概率，在工业物联网（IIoT）资源受限且网络质量波动的环境下，实现信任收敛速度最高提升28.6%，同时增强对恶意节点的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信任模型在IIoT环境中忽略了网络质量波动的影响，导致信任收敛速度慢、评估不准确，无法适应资源受限设备和动态恶劣网络条件的需求。

Method: 集成机器学习预测信任收敛所需时间单位，基于关键网络指标动态调整信任模型中的转移概率，并在基于IEEE 802.11标准的真实Wi-Fi信道条件下进行仿真验证。

Result: 在恶劣网络条件下，信任收敛时间最高减少28.6%；在存在恶意节点的场景中，信任评估准确性得到提升。

Conclusion: TCA方法为IIoT系统提供了一个可扩展、自适应的信任框架，能够在动态工业环境中确保在变化网络条件下的稳健性能。

Abstract: In Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) environments, trust management plays a vital role in securing systems, especially when dealing with resource-constrained devices. Traditional trust models often overlook the impact of fluctuating network quality, leading to slower trust convergence and inaccurate assessments. In this paper, we propose a dynamic trust management solution, known as the Trust Convergence Acceleration (TCA) approach, which integrates Machine Learning (ML) to accelerate trust convergence under poor network conditions. Our model predicts the number of time units needed for trust convergence based on key network metrics and dynamically adapts transition probabilities in the trust model to enhance convergence speed. Using a simulation framework that incorporates realistic Wi-Fi channel conditions based on the IEEE 802.11 standard, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the TCA-based approach, achieving up to a 28.6% reduction in trust convergence time under challenging conditions. Furthermore, the proposed solution exhibits resilience in scenarios involving malicious nodes, improving trust evaluation accuracy. This work provides a scalable and adaptive trust framework for IIoT systems in dynamic industrial environments, ensuring robust performance under varying network conditions.

</details>


### [216] [GNSS Spoofing Threat for V2X communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20215)
*Adolfo P. Jimenez,Juan Arquero-Gallego,Mario P. Luna,Jose E. Naranjo,Felipe Jimenez Alonso*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种使用低成本SDR（HackRF One）对V2X通信中的GNSS接收机进行物理欺骗攻击的方法，通过生成高保真GPS基带信号，成功使商用OBU和RSU设备在三种速度场景下产生虚假位置，揭示了V2X通信系统在GNSS欺骗攻击下的脆弱性且无法被检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS信号极易受到欺骗攻击，而V2X通信依赖GNSS提供的关键PNT服务生成协作感知消息（CAM），现有研究尚未充分验证低成本SDR设备对真实商用V2X设备的欺骗攻击效果及攻击的可检测性。

Method: 提出了一种坐标生成流水线，包括基于Haversine公式的距离计算、模拟匀速运动的时间离散化、以及线性插值生成高保真GPS基带信号；使用HackRF One SDR在Commsignia OBU和RSU设备上，针对90 km/h、145 km/h和200 km/h三种合成轨迹场景进行实验验证。

Result: 成功在三种速度场景下实现了对真实商用V2X设备的GNSS欺骗攻击，导致设备生成虚假位置，且攻击未被检测到。

Conclusion: V2X通信系统存在严重安全漏洞，其GNSS接收机易受低成本欺骗攻击，导致服务降级且无法被检测，亟需加强安全防护机制。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) constitute a core technology for delivering crucial positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) services in the Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) domain, where they are indispensable for generating Cooperative Awareness Messages (CAM) that uphold network reliability and vehicular safety. Yet, GNSS signals are acutely exposed to spoofing, an advanced attack in which an adversary transmits crafted signals that replicate legitimate satellite characteristics, misleading the receiver into computing a false position. This work presents a methodology for conducting physical spoofing with inexpensive Software Defined Radio (SDR), describing a coordinate generation pipeline that employs Haversine-based distance calculations, temporal discretization to emulate constant velocity, and linear interpolation to produce high-fidelity GPS baseband signals. The proposed attack is experimentally validated on real Commsignia OnBoard Unit (OBU) and RoadSide Unit (RSU) devices using a HackRF One across three scenarios that emulate synthetic trajectories at steady speeds of 90 km/h, 145 km/h, and 200 km/h. The most significant contribution of this paper is the demonstration that V2X communications are not secured, as they are susceptible to GNSS spoofing attacks, which cause service degradation without being detected.

</details>


### [217] [TrustMix: How to Mix Messages in a Mobile Ad-hoc Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20251)
*Yu Shen,Aiswarya Walter,Stefanie Roos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustMix 是一种无需中心可信方、适用于移动自组织网络（MANETs）的混合协议，通过分组转发与组内洗牌实现消息匿名，并利用可链接环签名实施速率限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络主要面向基础设施网络，无法直接应用于移动自组织网络（MANETs）；少数已有方案需要预先了解拓扑结构或依赖中心可信方，这在 MANETs 中难以实现。

Method: 用户寻找附近可信节点加入其组，消息经由多个组转发；每个组内所有成员共同参与消息洗牌（shuffling），使得原始消息与转发消息不可链接；使用可链接环签名对消息数量进行速率限制，在检测超额发送的同时不泄露身份。

Result: 在随机预言机模型下证明了协议安全性；通过现有混合网络模拟器评估，TrustMix 显著提升了消息匿名性；在 5 台移动设备上的概念验证 Android 实现显示了可接受的吞吐量。

Conclusion: TrustMix 是在 MANETs 中无需中心可信方即可实现匿名通信的有效方案，兼顾了安全性、匿名性和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Mix networks are a highly effective way to achieve anonymity, defending against a wide range of traffic-analysis attacks. However, mix networks are usually designed for infrastructure networks and cannot be directly applied in the context of mobile ad hoc networks (MANETs). The few existing solutions for MANETs require advance knowledge of the topology or a trusted central party.
  In this paper, we present TrustMix, a mix protocol for MANETs that operates without any central trusted party. In TrustMix, parties join groups and then messages are forwarded via multiple groups to provide anonymity. With TrustMix, users only need to find a party nearby that they consider trusted. They then forward the message to this party's group, and the party shuffles messages before forwarding to other groups, meaning that the original message and the forwarded message cannot be linked. Furthermore, even if the chosen party is adversarial, they can only break the anonymity if all parties in their group are adversarial as all of them contribute to the shuffling. In addition to anonymity, TrustMix also enforces rate limits on the number of messages through the use of linkable ring signatures, which allows detecting that parties send more messages that allowed without revealing identities.
  We prove the security of our protocol in the random oracle model. We evaluate its anonymity using an existing mix-network simulator and show that TrustMix significantly improves message anonymity. Finally, we present a proof-of-concept Android implementation and show that TrustMix achieves acceptable throughput with 5 mobile devices.

</details>


### [218] [Quantization as a Malicious Task: Removing Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors via Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20254)
*Kaihsun Yang,Min-Yan Tsai,Chia-Mu Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QVec，一种从参数空间视角防御量化条件后门的方法。QVec通过分析全精度模型与量化模型之间的权重差异，将其解释为恶意任务向量，并在部署前进行参数修正，无需重训练、触发样本或特定量化设置，仅需单次量化过程和轻量级超参数搜索。实验在图像分类和LLM攻击场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化条件后门防御方法通常需要修改量化流程或矫正激活统计量，引入额外计算开销且依赖特定量化设置，缺乏高效、通用的防御方案。

Method: QVec从参数空间视角出发，将全精度模型与量化模型间的权重差异视为结构化行为偏移（即恶意任务向量），通过受控参数修正来抵消该恶意方向。仅需单次量化评估参数偏移，结合轻量级超参数搜索，无需重训练和触发样本。

Result: 在多个图像分类基准和多种大语言模型攻击场景下，QVec持续抑制后门激活，同时保持干净性能不受影响。

Conclusion: QVec是一种高效、通用且轻量的防御方案，从参数空间重新解释了量化条件后门的本质，为模型量化安全提供了新视角。

Abstract: Model quantization is widely adopted to reduce memory usage and inference cost when deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained devices. However, recent studies have revealed a new security threat known as Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors (QCBs), where a model behaves normally in full precision but activates malicious behavior only after quantization. Existing defenses typically modify quantization procedures or correct activation statistics, often introducing additional computational overhead or relying on specific quantization settings. Here, we present QVec, a parameter-space perspective for defending against QCBs. We observe that the weight difference between a full-precision model and its quantized counterpart encodes a structured behavioral shift, which can be interpreted as a malicious task vector rather than random quantization noise. Based on this insight, QVec counteracts this malicious direction through controlled parameter correction prior to deployment. QVec requires no retraining, no trigger samples, and only a single quantization pass to estimate the parameter shift, together with a lightweight hyperparameter search. Extensive experiments across image classification benchmarks and multiple Large Language Model (LLM) attack scenarios demonstrate that QVec consistently suppresses backdoor activation while preserving clean performance.

</details>


### [219] [LLM agent safety, multi-turn red-teaming, jailbreak benchmarks, adversarial robustness, safety-critical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20408)
*Hanwool Lee,Dasol Choi,Bokyeong Kim,Seung Geun Kim,Haon Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NRT-Bench，一个用于多轮红队测试LLM智能体在安全关键系统中鲁棒性的基准测试平台。该平台模拟核电站控制室场景，在受控多轮攻击会话中，四种前沿模型有8.7%-12.1%的概率导致安全功能丧失，且不同模型的漏洞几乎不重叠。防御效果高度依赖模型，同一防御策略对不同模型的效果可能截然相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正被越来越多地用于安全关键系统的监管组件，但它们在持续、自适应对抗压力下的鲁棒性尚未得到充分表征。现有评估缺乏客观的物理安全信号作为衡量标准，且缺少多轮、自适应攻击场景的基准测试。

Method: 构建NRT-Bench基准测试平台：(1) 模拟核电站控制室，包含由五个LLM智能体组成的操作员团队，管理六个关键安全功能(CSFs)；(2) 攻击者通过四个通信渠道注入消息，进行有界多轮对抗会话，每轮含反馈；(3) 采用固定攻击配对重播协议评估四种前沿模型；(4) 以CSF丢失作为客观危害信号，而非LLM判断的文本。

Result: (1) 四种模型在攻击下有8.7%-12.1%的会话导致CSF丢失；(2) 各模型漏洞几乎不重叠：149个攻击会话中，无任何一个能同时攻破所有四种模型，但三分之一能攻破至少一个；(3) 防御效果高度依赖模型，同一防护措施对一种模型降低攻击成功率，却可能提高另一种模型的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: LLM智能体在安全关键场景下的漏洞呈现模型特异性而非层级嵌套性，不同模型的安全缺陷相互正交；防御措施的迁移性极差，需要针对特定模型定制化设计；建议发布模拟环境和工具以支持可重复的安全评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed as supervisory components for safety-critical systems, yet their robustness under sustained, adaptive adversarial pressure remains poorly characterized. We present NRT-Bench, a benchmark for multi-turn red-teaming of LLM agents acting as operators of a safety-critical system, instantiated in a simulated nuclear power plant control room. A five-role operator team, each backed by a configurable LLM, runs a plant governed by six critical safety functions (CSFs), while adversaries inject messages over four channels in bounded multi-turn sessions with per-turn feedback. Harm is an objective signal rather than LLM-judged text: a run terminates the moment any CSF is lost, attributed to the causing message. Evaluating four frontier operator models under a fixed-attack paired-replay protocol, we find that adaptive multi-turn attacks reliably push the operator team past a safety limit: across the four models, between 8.7% and 12.1% of attack sessions end with the plant losing a critical safety function. Although the four models look almost equally robust by this aggregate rate, their failures barely overlap: of $149$ sessions, none defeat all four models while a third defeat at least one, so vulnerabilities are nearly disjoint across models rather than nested. The effect of added defences is strongly model-dependent: the same guardrail stack or safety-advisor agent that lowers attack success for one model can raise it for another. We release the simulation venue, attack dataset, and replay tooling for reproducible safety evaluation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [220] [Multi-View Decompilation for LLM-Based Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20436)
*Bercan Turkmen,Vyas Raina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出多反编译器提示方法，通过同时使用Ghidra和RetDec两种反编译器的伪C视图，提升LLM对恶意软件分类的召回率和F1分数，验证了不同反编译器输出具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的恶意软件反编译代码分类工作通常仅依赖单一反编译器视图，但反编译器是有损的启发式工具，不同反编译器会暴露同一二进制文件的不同特征，导致分类结果脆弱不可靠。

Method: 构建包含良性工具和恶意程序的基准数据集，每个样本分别用Ghidra和RetDec编译并反编译，得到匹配的伪C视图；在多个主流LLM家族上测试，对比单视图与双视图提示的分类效果。

Result: 提供双反编译器视图可提升恶意样本分类的F1分数，主要源于恶意样本召回率的提高；Ghidra和RetDec的误差部分不同，确证了反编译器输出提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 多反编译器提示是一种简单、无需训练的方法，能有效提升基于LLM的恶意软件分类在实际环境中的表现。

Abstract: Malware analysts often inspect compiled binaries through decompiled pseudo-C, when source code is unavailable. Recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) can assist this process by classifying decompiled code as benign or malicious, but existing pipelines typically rely on a single decompiler view. We argue that this assumption is fragile: decompilers are lossy heuristic tools, and different decompilers can expose different artefacts of the same binary. We curate a benchmark of benign utilities and malicious programs spanning a range of threat behaviors. Each sample is compiled and decompiled with both Ghidra and RetDec, yielding matched pseudo-C views. Across a range of LLMs from major model families, we find that providing both decompiler views improves malicious-class F1, mainly by increasing recall on malicious samples. Agreement analyses further show that Ghidra and RetDec make partially different errors, supporting the view that decompiler outputs provide complementary evidence. Our results suggest that multi-decompiler prompting is a simple, training-free way to improve LLM-based malware triage in practical settings.

</details>


### [221] [Image Encryption Algorithm Based on Convolutional Neural Networks and Dynamic S-Box Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20444)
*Ans Ibrahim,Fadhil Abbas Fadhil,Mahameed Reza Feizi Derakhshi,Maryam Mahdi Alhusseini,Nikolai Safiullin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合CNN与经典密码学的动态图像加密方法，通过CNN训练生成自适应S-box，提升非线性、唯一性和输入依赖性，增强混淆特性以抵抗线性、差分、统计和结构攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的固定S-box易受线性和差分攻击，缺乏对输入图像的适应性，安全性和灵活性不足。

Method: 利用CNN提取图像特征，基于学习到的特征生成自适应S-box，替代传统固定S-box进行像素替换加密。

Result: 通过熵、直方图分析、相关性、NPCR和UACI等安全性评估，证明CNN生成的S-box具有更高的非线性、唯一性和输入依赖性，方案比传统方法更具鲁棒性和灵活性。

Conclusion: CNN驱动的动态S-box能有效提升图像加密的安全性，抵抗多种攻击，是一种更优的加密方案。

Abstract: The paper proposes a dynamic approach to image encryption, combining the use of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and classical cryptography to improve the security and flexibility of image encryption. The main concept is to create adaptive Substitution boxes (S-boxes) based on characteristics that are learned by a trained CNN. The CNN-based S-boxes can be relied on for more non-linearity, uniqueness, and input image dependence than the conventional fixed S-boxes because they are susceptible to the linear and differential attacks.
